        choose.disabled = true;
        feedback.textContent = 'Uploading...';
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/cover', { method: 'POST', body: form });
          if (!response.ok) {
            feedback.textContent = response.status === 413
              ? 'Image too large (max 5 MB).'
              : response.status === 415
                ? 'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP or GIF image.'
                : response.status === 401
                  ? 'Please log in again.'
                  : 'Could not upload the wall picture.';
            return;
          }
          const result = await response.json();
          setHomeCover(result.avatar_url, 50, 50, 100, true);
          feedback.textContent = 'Image uploaded. Adjust its position, then save and close.';
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not upload homepage cover:', error);
          feedback.textContent = 'Could not upload. Check your connection and try again.';
        } finally {
          file.value = '';
          choose.disabled = false;
        }
      };
      remove.onclick = async () => {
        remove.disabled = true;
        feedback.textContent = '';
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/cover', { method: 'DELETE' });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
          setHomeCover(null);
          feedback.textContent = '';
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not remove homepage cover:', error);
          feedback.textContent = 'Could not remove the wall picture. Please try again.';
        } finally {
          remove.disabled = false;
        }
      };
    }

    function commentRow(c, p, onDeleted) {
      const row = el('div', undefined, 'row');
      row.append(userLink(c.user), document.createTextNode(' ' + c.body));
      if (p && c.id != null && currentUsername && (c.user === currentUsername || p.user === currentUsername)) {
        const del = el('button', 'âœ•', 'comment-delete-button');
        del.type = 'button';
        del.title = 'Delete comment';
        del.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Delete comment');
        del.onclick = async () => {
          if (!confirm('Delete this comment?')) return;
          del.disabled = true;
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/comments/' + c.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('status ' + response.status);
            row.remove();
            if (typeof onDeleted === 'function') onDeleted();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not delete comment:', error);
            del.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        row.append(del);
      }
      return row;
    }

    async function refreshActiveUsers() {
      const list = document.getElementById('activeUsersList');
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/users/active');
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Active users request failed: ' + response.status);
        const users = await response.json();
        list.textContent = '';
        if (users.length === 0) {
          list.append(el('p', 'No one is online just now.', 'active-users-empty'));
          return;
        }
        users.forEach(user => {
          const link = profileLink(user.username, '');
          link.className = 'active-user';
          link.setAttribute('aria-label', '@' + user.username + ' is online');
          const avatar = el('span', undefined, 'active-user-avatar');
          avatar.append(avatarWithMood(user.avatar_url, user.mood));
          link.append(avatar, el('span', user.username, 'active-user-name'));
          list.append(link);
        });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not refresh active users:', error);
        list.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Online people could not be loaded. Retrying shortly.', 'active-users-empty'));
      }
    }

    async function refreshPresence() {
      const status = document.getElementById('activePresenceStatus');
      if (loggedIn) {
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/presence', { method: 'POST' });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Presence update failed: ' + response.status);
          status.hidden = true;
          status.textContent = '';
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not update online presence:', error);
          status.hidden = false;
          status.textContent = 'Your online status could not be refreshed.';
        }
      } else {
        status.hidden = true;
        status.textContent = '';
      }
      await refreshActiveUsers();
    }

    function startPresenceTracking() {
      if (presenceInterval !== null) clearInterval(presenceInterval);
      refreshPresence();
      presenceInterval = setInterval(refreshPresence, 30000);
    }

    // ---------- routing ----------
    function route() {
      const h = location.hash;
      document.getElementById('friendsView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('communityView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('adminView').hidden = !h.startsWith('#/admin');
      if (h.startsWith('#/u/')) {
        const [username, dashboardSection] = h.slice(4).split('#', 2);
        showProfile(decodeURIComponent(username), false, dashboardSection || null);
      } else if (h === '#/inbox') {
        showInbox();
      } else if (h === '#/friends') {
        showFriends();
      } else if (h === '#/community' || h.startsWith('#/community/')) {
        const id = h.startsWith('#/community/') ? Number(h.slice('#/community/'.length)) : null;
        if (id && Number.isInteger(id)) showCommunityDetail(id); else showCommunities();
      } else if (h === '#/admin') {
        showAdmin();
      } else if (h === '#/search') {
        showUserSearch();
      } else if (h === '#/settings' || h.startsWith('#/settings#')) {
        showSettings(h.split('#')[2] || null);
      } else if (h === '#/signup') {
        showSignup();
      } else {
        showHome();
      }
    }

    async function showAdmin() {
      stopInboxPolling();
      ['homeView', 'profileView', 'inboxView', 'userSearchView', 'settingsView', 'signupView']
        .forEach(id => { document.getElementById(id).hidden = true; });
      document.getElementById('appPausedView').hidden = true;
      const view = document.getElementById('adminView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.replaceChildren(el('h1', 'Admin hub', 'inbox-title'));
      const status = el('p', 'Loading admin dataâ€¦', 'empty');
      view.append(status);
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/admin/overview');
        if (response.status === 403) throw new Error('This account is not an administrator. Set ADMIN_USERNAMES in the app environment and restart the server.');
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error(response.status === 401 ? 'Please sign in again.' : 'Admin data could not be loaded.');
        const data = await response.json();
        currentIsAdmin = true;
        currentIsSuperadmin = data.is_superadmin;
        const grid = el('div', undefined, 'admin-grid');
        [['Users', data.total_users, 'admin-users'], ['Paused accounts', data.disabled_users, 'admin-users'], ['Posts', data.total_posts, 'admin-posts'], ['Messages', data.total_messages, 'admin-conversations'], ['Chats', data.total_conversations, 'admin-conversations'], ['Group chats', data.total_groups, 'admin-conversations'], ['Reports', data.total_reports, 'admin-reports']]
          .forEach(([label, value, target]) => {
            const card = el('button', undefined, 'admin-stat');
            card.type = 'button';
            card.title = 'Open ' + label.toLowerCase() + ' details';
            card.onclick = () => document.getElementById(target)?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
            card.append(el('strong', String(value)), el('span', label + ' Â· View details â†’'));
            grid.append(card);
          });
        const config = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        config.append(el('h2', 'App settings and configuration'));
        config.append(el('p', 'Server: Rust + Axum Â· Database: PostgreSQL Â· Uploads: images and video Â· Session lifetime: 7 days', 'admin-muted'));
        config.append(el('p', 'Admins can review app data and pause accounts. Superadmins can change app availability, manage admins, remove accounts, and delete any chat.', 'admin-muted'));
        const signupLabel = el('label');
        const signupToggle = el('input'); signupToggle.type = 'checkbox'; signupToggle.checked = data.config.allow_signups;
        signupLabel.append(signupToggle, document.createTextNode(' Allow new account signups'));
        config.append(signupLabel);
        let appToggle = null;
        if (data.is_superadmin) {
          const appLabel = el('label');
          appToggle = el('input'); appToggle.type = 'checkbox'; appToggle.checked = data.config.app_enabled;
          appLabel.append(appToggle, document.createTextNode(' Keep the app enabled for everyone'));
          config.append(appLabel);
        }
        const saveConfig = el('button', 'Save configuration'); saveConfig.type = 'button';
        const configStatus = el('span', '', 'admin-muted');
        saveConfig.onclick = async () => {
          saveConfig.disabled = true;
          const saved = await fetch('/api/admin/config', {
            method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify({ allow_signups: signupToggle.checked, ...(appToggle ? { app_enabled: appToggle.checked } : {}) }),
          });
          saveConfig.disabled = false;
          configStatus.textContent = saved.ok ? 'Saved.' : saved.status === 403 ? 'Only a superadmin can change app availability.' : 'Could not save this setting.';
        };
        config.append(saveConfig, configStatus);
        const adminsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        adminsPanel.id = 'admin-manage-admins';
        const adminUsers = data.users.filter(user => user.is_admin || user.is_superadmin);
        adminsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Admin access'));
        adminsPanel.append(el('p', 'Only superadmins can grant or remove admin access. Admin-only pages and APIs stay hidden from regular members.', 'admin-muted'));
        const adminsList = el('p', adminUsers.length ? adminUsers.map(user => '@' + user.username + (user.is_superadmin ? ' Â· Superadmin' : ' Â· Admin')).join('  |  ') : 'No administrators found.', 'admin-muted');
        adminsPanel.append(adminsList);
        const eligibleAdmins = data.users.filter(user => !user.is_admin && !user.is_superadmin && user.account_enabled);
        if (data.is_superadmin) {
          if (eligibleAdmins.length) {
            const addRow = el('div');
            const select = el('select');
            select.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Choose a user to promote to admin');
            select.append(new Option('Choose a userâ€¦', ''));
            eligibleAdmins.forEach(user => select.append(new Option('@' + user.username, String(user.id))));
            const grant = el('button', 'Add admin'); grant.type = 'button';
            grant.onclick = async () => {
              if (!select.value) { alert('Choose a user first.'); return; }
              grant.disabled = true;
              const result = await fetch('/api/admin/users/' + select.value + '/role', {
                method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ is_admin: true }),
              });
              if (result.ok) showAdmin();
              else { grant.disabled = false; alert('Could not grant admin access.'); }
            };
            addRow.append(select, grant); adminsPanel.append(addRow);
          } else {
            adminsPanel.append(el('p', 'Every enabled account already has admin access.'));
          }
          const removeInfo = el('p', 'To remove an admin, use â€œRemove adminâ€ beside their account below.', 'admin-muted');
          adminsPanel.append(removeInfo);
        }
        const usersPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        usersPanel.id = 'admin-users';
        usersPanel.append(el('h2', 'Accounts and user settings'));
        const table = el('table', undefined, 'admin-table');
        const head = el('tr');
        ['User', 'Contact', 'Role / account', 'Profile data', 'Privacy settings', 'Avatar / cover', 'Cover position', 'Posts', 'Joined', 'Action'].forEach(label => head.append(el('th', label)));
        const thead = el('thead'); thead.append(head); table.append(thead);
        const tbody = el('tbody');
        data.users.forEach(user => {
          const row = el('tr');
          const profileData = [user.display_name, user.age ? user.age + ' years' : '', user.date_of_birth ? 'Birthday ' + user.date_of_birth : '', user.company_name, user.designation, user.location, user.mood, user.interests, user.relationship_status, user.bio]
            .filter(Boolean).join(' Â· ') || 'â€”';
          const privacy = [user.profile_visibility, 'messages: ' + user.message_privacy, 'birthday: ' + user.birthday_visibility, 'work: ' + user.work_visibility, 'email: ' + user.email_visibility, 'phone: ' + user.phone_visibility, 'social: ' + user.social_visibility,
            'activity: ' + (user.show_activity_status ? 'shown' : 'hidden'),
            'visitors: ' + (user.keep_profile_visitors ? 'saved' : 'off'),
            'mentions: ' + (user.notify_mentions ? 'on' : 'off')].join(' Â· ');
          row.append(el('td', '@' + user.username + (user.display_name ? ' Â· ' + user.display_name : '')),
            el('td', [user.email ? 'Login: ' + user.email : '', user.contact_email ? 'Contact: ' + user.contact_email + ' (' + user.email_visibility + ')' : '', user.phone_number ? 'Phone: ' + user.phone_number + ' (' + user.phone_visibility + ')' : '', [user.instagram_handle, user.facebook_handle, user.tiktok_handle, user.linkedin_handle].filter(Boolean).length ? 'Social: ' + [user.instagram_handle, user.facebook_handle, user.tiktok_handle, user.linkedin_handle].filter(Boolean).join(', ') + ' (' + user.social_visibility + ')' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' Â· ') || 'â€”'),
            el('td', (user.is_superadmin ? 'Superadmin' : user.is_admin ? 'Admin' : 'Member') + ' Â· ' + (user.account_enabled ? 'Enabled' : 'Paused')),
            el('td', profileData), el('td', privacy),
            el('td', [user.avatar_url ? 'Avatar' : '', user.cover_image_url ? 'Cover' : ''].filter(Boolean).join(' Â· ') || 'â€”'),
            el('td', user.cover_position_x + '% / ' + user.cover_position_y + '% Â· ' + user.cover_zoom + '%'),
            el('td', String(user.post_count)), el('td', new Date(user.created_at).toLocaleDateString()));
          const actionCell = el('td');
          const toggle = el('button', user.account_enabled ? 'Pause account' : 'Enable account');
          toggle.disabled = user.username === currentUsername;
          toggle.title = toggle.disabled ? 'Admins cannot pause their own account here.' : '';
          toggle.onclick = async () => {
            toggle.disabled = true;
            const updated = await fetch('/api/admin/users/' + user.id, {
              method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
              body: JSON.stringify({ account_enabled: !user.account_enabled }),
            });
            if (updated.ok) showAdmin();
            else { toggle.disabled = false; alert('Could not update this account.'); }
          };
          actionCell.append(toggle);
          if (data.is_superadmin && user.username !== currentUsername) {
            const role = el('button', user.is_admin ? 'Remove admin' : 'Make admin');
            role.onclick = async () => {
              role.disabled = true;
              const updated = await fetch('/api/admin/users/' + user.id + '/role', {
                method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
                body: JSON.stringify({ is_admin: !user.is_admin }),
              });
              if (updated.ok) showAdmin();
              else { role.disabled = false; alert('Could not update admin access.'); }
            };
            const removeUser = el('button', 'Remove user');
            removeUser.onclick = async () => {
              if (!confirm('Permanently remove @' + user.username + ' and their account data?')) return;
              removeUser.disabled = true;
              const removed = await fetch('/api/admin/users/' + user.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
              if (removed.ok) showAdmin();
              else { removeUser.disabled = false; alert('Could not remove this account.'); }
            };
            actionCell.append(role, removeUser);
          }
          row.append(actionCell); tbody.append(row);
        });
        table.append(tbody); usersPanel.append(table);
        const chatsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        chatsPanel.id = 'admin-conversations';
        chatsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Chats and conversations'), el('p', 'Showing up to 500 newest chats. Deleting a chat removes it and its messages for every member.', 'admin-muted'));
        if (data.conversations.length === 0) chatsPanel.append(el('p', 'No chats have been created.', 'admin-muted'));
        else {
          const chatsTable = el('table', undefined, 'admin-table');
          const chatsHead = el('tr');
          ['Chat', 'Owner', 'Members', 'Latest message', 'Started', 'Moderation', ...(data.is_superadmin ? ['Action'] : [])].forEach(label => chatsHead.append(el('th', label)));
          const chatsThead = el('thead'); chatsThead.append(chatsHead); chatsTable.append(chatsThead);
          const chatsBody = el('tbody');
          data.conversations.forEach(chat => {
            const row = el('tr');
            row.append(el('td', (chat.is_group ? 'Group Â· ' : 'Direct Â· ') + (chat.title || '#' + chat.id)),
              el('td', '@' + chat.owner), el('td', String(chat.member_count)),
              el('td', chat.latest_message || 'â€”'), el('td', new Date(chat.created_at).toLocaleString()));
            const moderation = el('td');
            moderation.append(el('span', chat.is_muted ? 'Muted' : 'Active', 'admin-muted'));
            const mute = el('button', chat.is_muted ? 'Unmute chat' : 'Mute chat');
            mute.onclick = async () => {
              mute.disabled = true;
              const result = await fetch('/api/admin/conversations/' + chat.id + '/moderation', {
                method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ is_muted: !chat.is_muted }),
              });
              if (result.ok) showAdmin();
              else { mute.disabled = false; alert('Could not update chat moderation.'); }
            };
            moderation.append(document.createTextNode(' '), mute); row.append(moderation);
            if (data.is_superadmin) {
              const action = el('td');
              const remove = el('button', 'Delete chat');
              remove.onclick = async () => {
                if (!confirm('Delete this chat and all its messages for every member?')) return;
                remove.disabled = true;
                const result = await fetch('/api/admin/conversations/' + chat.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (result.ok) showAdmin();
                else { remove.disabled = false; alert('Could not delete this chat.'); }
              };
              action.append(remove); row.append(action);
            }
            chatsBody.append(row);
          });
          chatsTable.append(chatsBody); chatsPanel.append(chatsTable);
        }
        const postsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        postsPanel.id = 'admin-posts';
        postsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Recent posts'), el('p', 'Showing up to 500 newest posts.', 'admin-muted'));
        if (data.posts.length === 0) postsPanel.append(el('p', 'No posts have been published.', 'admin-muted'));
        else {
          const postsTable = el('table', undefined, 'admin-table');
          const postHead = el('tr');
          ['ID', 'Author', 'Caption', 'Media', 'Posted'].forEach(label => postHead.append(el('th', label)));
          const postThead = el('thead'); postThead.append(postHead); postsTable.append(postThead);
          const postBody = el('tbody');
          data.posts.forEach(post => {
            const row = el('tr');
            row.append(el('td', '#' + post.id), el('td', '@' + post.author), el('td', post.caption || 'â€”'));
            const mediaCell = el('td'); const mediaLink = el('a', 'Open ' + post.media_type);
            mediaLink.href = post.image_url; mediaLink.target = '_blank'; mediaLink.rel = 'noopener';
            mediaCell.append(mediaLink); row.append(mediaCell, el('td', new Date(post.created_at).toLocaleString()));
            postBody.append(row);
          });
          postsTable.append(postBody); postsPanel.append(postsTable);
        }
        const reportsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        reportsPanel.id = 'admin-reports';
        reportsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Recent post reports'));
        if (data.reports.length === 0) reportsPanel.append(el('p', 'No reports have been submitted.', 'admin-muted'));
        else {
          const reports = el('table', undefined, 'admin-table');
          const reportHead = el('tr');
          ['Reporter', 'Post', 'Reason', 'Details', 'Reported'].forEach(label => reportHead.append(el('th', label)));
          const reportThead = el('thead'); reportThead.append(reportHead); reports.append(reportThead);
          const reportBody = el('tbody');
          data.reports.forEach(report => {
            const row = el('tr');
            [('@' + report.reporter), ('#' + report.post_id + ' Â· @' + report.post_author), report.reason, report.details || 'â€”', new Date(report.created_at).toLocaleString()]
              .forEach(value => row.append(el('td', value)));
            reportBody.append(row);
          });
          reports.append(reportBody); reportsPanel.append(reports);
        }
        const chatReportsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'admin-panel');
        chatReportsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Recent chat reports'));
        if (data.chat_reports.length === 0) chatReportsPanel.append(el('p', 'No chat reports have been submitted.', 'admin-muted'));
        else {
          const reports = el('table', undefined, 'admin-table');
          const reportHead = el('tr');
          ['Reporter', 'Conversation', 'Details', 'Reported'].forEach(label => reportHead.append(el('th', label)));
          const reportThead = el('thead'); reportThead.append(reportHead); reports.append(reportThead);
          const reportBody = el('tbody');
          data.chat_reports.forEach(report => {
            const row = el('tr');
            [('@' + report.reporter), ('Conversation #' + report.conversation_id), report.details || 'â€”', new Date(report.created_at).toLocaleString()]
              .forEach(value => row.append(el('td', value)));
            reportBody.append(row);
          });
          reports.append(reportBody); chatReportsPanel.append(reports);
        }
        view.replaceChildren(el('h1', data.is_superadmin ? 'Superadmin dashboard' : 'Admin hub', 'inbox-title'), grid, config, ...(data.is_superadmin ? [adminsPanel] : []), usersPanel, chatsPanel, postsPanel, reportsPanel, chatReportsPanel);
      } catch (error) {
        status.className = 'empty';
        status.textContent = error.message;
      }
    }

    function hideSocialViews() {
      ['homeView', 'profileView', 'inboxView', 'friendsView', 'communityView', 'userSearchView', 'settingsView', 'signupView', 'adminView']
        .forEach(id => { const node = document.getElementById(id); if (node) node.hidden = true; });
      document.getElementById('appPausedView').hidden = true;
    }

    async function showFriends() {
      stopInboxPolling(); hideSocialViews();
      const view = document.getElementById('friendsView'); view.hidden = false; view.replaceChildren();
      view.append(el('h1', 'Friends', 'inbox-title'));
      if (!loggedIn) { view.append(el('p', 'Log in to see the people you follow.', 'empty')); return; }
      const response = await fetch('/api/me/friends');
      if (!response.ok) { view.append(el('p', 'Friends could not be loaded. Please try again.', 'empty')); return; }
      const people = await response.json();
      const following = el('section'); following.append(el('h2', `Following Â· ${people.length}`));
      if (!people.length) following.append(el('p', 'People you follow will appear here.', 'empty'));
      const grid = el('div', undefined, 'social-grid');
      people.forEach(person => {
        const card = el('article', undefined, 'social-card');
        const row = el('div', undefined, 'social-person'); row.append(avatarEl(person.avatar_url), profileLink(person.username, '@' + person.username)); card.append(row);
        const actions = el('div', undefined, 'social-card-actions');
        const favorite = el('button', person.is_favorite ? 'â˜… Favorited' : 'â˜† Add to favorites');
        favorite.onclick = async () => { const r = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(person.username) + '/favorite', { method: person.is_favorite ? 'DELETE' : 'POST' }); if (r.ok) showFriends(); };
        const unfollow = el('button', 'Unfollow');
        unfollow.onclick = async () => { if (window.confirm(`Unfollow @${person.username}?`)) { const r = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(person.username) + '/follow', { method: 'DELETE' }); if (r.ok) showFriends(); } };
        actions.append(favorite, unfollow); card.append(actions); grid.append(card);
      });
      following.append(grid); view.append(following);
      const favoriteSection = el('section'); favoriteSection.append(el('h2', 'Favorites'));
      const favorites = people.filter(p => p.is_favorite);
      if (!favorites.length) favoriteSection.append(el('p', 'Mark people as favorites for quick access.', 'empty'));
      const favGrid = el('div', undefined, 'social-grid');
      favorites.forEach(person => { const card = el('article', undefined, 'social-card'); const row = el('div', undefined, 'social-person'); row.append(avatarEl(person.avatar_url), profileLink(person.username, '@' + person.username)); card.append(row); favGrid.append(card); });
      favoriteSection.append(favGrid); view.append(favoriteSection);
    }

    function communitySideItem(group, activeId, openCommunity) {
      const button = el('button', undefined, 'community-side-item' + (Number(activeId) === group.id ? ' active' : ''));
      button.type = 'button';
      const mark = group.image_url ? el('img') : el('span', group.name.slice(0, 1).toUpperCase(), 'community-side-mark');
      if (group.image_url) { mark.src = group.image_url; mark.alt = ''; }
      const copy = el('span', undefined, 'community-side-copy');
      copy.append(el('strong', group.name), el('span', group.is_disabled ? 'Access paused' : `${group.member_count} members`));
      button.append(mark, copy);
      button.disabled = group.is_disabled;
      button.onclick = () => openCommunity(group);
      return button;
    }

    function communityWorkspace(view, communities, activeId) {
      const workspace = el('div', undefined, 'community-workspace');
      const sidebar = el('aside', undefined, 'community-sidebar');
      const sidebarHead = el('div', undefined, 'community-sidebar-head');
      sidebarHead.append(el('h2', 'Your spaces'));
      const create = el('button', '+ Create'); create.type = 'button';
      sidebarHead.append(create); sidebar.append(sidebarHead);
      const createForm = el('form', undefined, 'community-form'); createForm.hidden = true;
      const name = el('input'); name.placeholder = 'Community name'; name.maxLength = 50; name.required = true;
      const description = el('textarea'); description.placeholder = 'What brings this community together?'; description.maxLength = 500;
      const image = el('input'); image.type = 'file'; image.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif';
      const imageLabel = el('label', 'Community image Â· optional', 'community-create-image'); imageLabel.append(image);
      const createStatus = el('p', '', 'community-note'); const submit = el('button', 'Create community'); submit.type = 'submit';
      createForm.append(name, description, imageLabel, submit, createStatus);
      create.onclick = () => { createForm.hidden = !createForm.hidden; };
      createForm.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault(); submit.disabled = true; createStatus.textContent = 'Creatingâ€¦';
        try {
          const response = await post('/api/communities', { name: name.value, description: description.value });
          if (!response.ok) { createStatus.textContent = response.status === 409 ? 'That name is already in use.' : 'Could not create the community.'; return; }
          const created = await response.json();
          if (image.files && image.files[0]) {
            const form = new FormData(); form.append('image', image.files[0]);
            const uploaded = await fetch(`/api/communities/${created.id}/image`, { method: 'POST', body: form });
            if (!uploaded.ok) alert(uploaded.status === 413 ? 'Community created, but its image exceeds 5 MB.' : 'Community created, but its image could not be uploaded. You can add it from the community header.');
          }
          location.hash = '#/community/' + created.id;
        } catch (error) { createStatus.textContent = 'Could not create the community. Check your connection and try again.'; }
        finally { submit.disabled = false; }
      };
      sidebar.append(createForm);
      const joined = communities.filter(group => group.is_member || group.is_disabled);
      const mine = el('section', undefined, 'community-sidebar-section');
      mine.append(el('h3', 'Joined'));
      const joinedList = el('div', undefined, 'community-side-list');
      if (!joined.length) joinedList.append(el('p', 'Your joined communities will appear here.', 'community-note'));
      joined.forEach(group => joinedList.append(communitySideItem(group, activeId, item => { if (!item.is_disabled) location.hash = '#/community/' + item.id; })));
      mine.append(joinedList); sidebar.append(mine);
      const discover = el('section', undefined, 'community-sidebar-section'); discover.append(el('h3', 'Discover'));
      const discoverList = el('div', undefined, 'community-side-list');
      const available = communities.filter(group => !group.is_member && !group.is_disabled);
      if (!available.length) discoverList.append(el('p', 'You are up to date.', 'community-note'));
      available.forEach(group => discoverList.append(communitySideItem(group, activeId, async item => {
        const response = await post(`/api/communities/${item.id}/join`);
        if (response.ok) location.hash = '#/community/' + item.id;
        else alert(response.status === 403 ? 'Your access to this community is paused. Contact its owner.' : 'Could not join this community.');
      })));
      discover.append(discoverList); sidebar.append(discover);
      const main = el('main', undefined, 'community-main');
      workspace.append(sidebar, main); view.append(workspace);
      return main;
    }

    async function showCommunities() {
      stopInboxPolling(); hideSocialViews();
      const view = document.getElementById('communityView'); view.hidden = false; view.replaceChildren();
      if (!loggedIn) { view.append(el('p', 'Log in to join or create a community.', 'empty')); return; }
      const response = await fetch('/api/communities');
      if (!response.ok) { view.append(el('p', 'Communities could not be loaded.', 'empty')); return; }
      const communities = await response.json();
      const main = communityWorkspace(view, communities, null);
      const empty = el('section', undefined, 'community-main-empty');
      empty.append(el('h1', 'Find your people'), el('p', 'Choose a community on the left to read discussions, share ideas, and meet members.'));
      main.append(empty);
    }

    async function showCommunityDetail(id) {
      stopInboxPolling(); hideSocialViews();
      const view = document.getElementById('communityView'); view.hidden = false; view.replaceChildren();
      if (!loggedIn) { view.append(el('p', 'Log in to view communities.', 'empty')); return; }
      const listRes = await fetch('/api/communities');
      if (!listRes.ok) { view.append(el('p', 'Community could not be loaded.', 'empty')); return; }
      const communities = await listRes.json();
      const group = communities.find(item => item.id === id);
      const main = communityWorkspace(view, communities, id);
      if (!group) { main.append(el('p', 'Community not found.', 'empty')); return; }
      const banner = el('section', undefined, 'community-banner');
      if (group.image_url) { const cover = el('img'); cover.className = 'community-banner-image'; cover.src = group.image_url; cover.alt = ''; banner.append(cover); }
      const bannerContent = el('div', undefined, 'community-banner-content');
      const copy = el('div', undefined, 'community-banner-copy');
      copy.append(el('h1', group.name), el('p', group.description || 'A place to connect, share what you know, and build community together.'), el('div', `${group.member_count} members${group.owner ? ' Â· owned by @' + group.owner : ' Â· owner not assigned'}${group.is_owner ? ' Â· you are the owner' : group.is_admin ? ' Â· community admin' : ''}`, 'community-banner-meta'));
      const actions = el('div', undefined, 'community-banner-actions');
      if (group.is_disabled) {
        actions.append(el('span', 'Your access is paused by the community owner.'));
      } else if (!group.is_member) {
        const join = el('button', 'Join community', 'community-join-button'); join.onclick = async () => { const result = await post(`/api/communities/${id}/join`); if (result.ok) showCommunityDetail(id); else alert(result.status === 403 ? 'Your access to this community is paused. Contact its owner.' : 'Could not join this community.'); };
        actions.append(join);
      } else if (group.is_member && !group.owner) {
        const claim = el('button', 'Become owner'); claim.onclick = async () => { if (!window.confirm('Become the owner of this community? You will control its settings and moderation.')) return; const result = await post(`/api/communities/${id}/claim`); if (result.ok) showCommunityDetail(id); else alert(result.status === 409 ? 'Someone else has already claimed ownership.' : 'Could not claim this community.'); };
        actions.append(claim);
      } else if (group.is_owner || group.is_admin) {
        const edit = el('button', 'Edit details'); edit.onclick = async () => { const name = prompt('Community name', group.name); if (!name) return; const description = prompt('Description', group.description) ?? group.description; const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ name, description }) }); if (result.ok) showCommunityDetail(id); else alert('Could not update the community.'); };
        const imageInput = el('input'); imageInput.type = 'file'; imageInput.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif'; imageInput.hidden = true;
        const imageButton = el('button', group.image_url ? 'Change image' : 'Add image'); imageButton.onclick = () => imageInput.click();
        imageInput.onchange = async () => { if (!imageInput.files || !imageInput.files[0]) return; if (imageInput.files[0].size > 5 * 1024 * 1024) { alert('Choose an image under 5 MB.'); imageInput.value = ''; return; } const form = new FormData(); form.append('image', imageInput.files[0]); const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/image`, { method: 'POST', body: form }); if (result.ok) showCommunityDetail(id); else alert(result.status === 415 ? 'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP, or GIF image.' : 'Could not upload the community image.'); };
        const memberAction = el('button', 'Manage members'); memberAction.onclick = async () => showCommunityTab('Members');
        if (group.is_owner) { const remove = el('button', 'Delete community'); remove.onclick = async () => { if (!window.confirm('Delete this community and its discussions?')) return; const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); if (result.ok) location.hash = '#/community'; else alert('Could not delete this community.'); }; actions.append(edit, imageButton, imageInput, remove); }
        actions.append(memberAction);
      } else if (group.is_member) {
        const leave = el('button', 'Leave community'); leave.onclick = async () => { const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/join`, { method: 'DELETE' }); if (result.ok) location.hash = '#/community'; else alert('Could not leave this community.'); };
        actions.append(leave);
      }
      bannerContent.append(copy, actions); banner.append(bannerContent); main.append(banner);
      if (!group.is_member) {
        main.append(el('section', 'Join this community to read discussions, reply, and share your ideas.', 'community-about-card'));
        return;
      }
      const tabBar = el('nav', undefined, 'community-tabs');
      const panel = el('section', undefined, 'community-tab-panel');
      const tabs = ['Discussion', 'About', 'Members'];
      function activateTab(name) {
        tabBar.querySelectorAll('.community-tab').forEach(button => { const active = button.textContent === name; button.classList.toggle('active', active); button.setAttribute('aria-current', active ? 'page' : 'false'); });
        panel.replaceChildren();
        if (name === 'About') {
          const about = el('article', undefined, 'community-about-card'); about.append(el('h2', 'About this community'), el('p', group.description || 'A place to connect, share what you know, and build community together.'), el('p', `${group.owner ? `Owned by @${group.owner}` : 'No owner yet'} Â· ${group.member_count} members`, 'community-meta')); panel.append(about); return;
        }
        if (name === 'Members') { loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel); return; }
        loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel);
      }
      tabs.forEach(name => { const button = el('button', name, 'community-tab'); button.type = 'button'; button.onclick = () => activateTab(name); tabBar.append(button); });
      main.append(tabBar, panel);
      activateTab('Discussion');
    }

    async function loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel) {
      panel.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading membersâ€¦', 'community-note'));
      const response = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/members`);
      if (!response.ok) {
        const message = response.status === 403
          ? 'Join this community to view its members.'
          : response.status === 404
            ? 'This community could not be found.'
            : 'The member list is temporarily unavailable. Please try again.';
        panel.replaceChildren(el('p', message, 'empty'));
        return;
      }
      const members = await response.json();
      const section = el('section', undefined, 'community-member-list'); section.append(el('h2', `Members Â· ${members.length}`));
      if (group.is_owner || group.is_admin) {
        const addForm = el('form', undefined, 'community-toolbar'); const username = el('input'); username.placeholder = 'Username to add'; username.maxLength = 30; username.required = true;
        const add = el('button', 'Add member'); add.type = 'submit'; const status = el('span', '', 'community-note'); addForm.append(username, add, status);
        addForm.onsubmit = async event => { event.preventDefault(); add.disabled = true; const result = await post(`/api/communities/${id}/members`, { username: username.value.trim() }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel); else status.textContent = result.status === 404 ? 'No enabled account has that username.' : 'Could not add that member.'; add.disabled = false; };
        section.append(addForm);
      }
      members.forEach(member => {
        const row = el('div', undefined, 'community-member-row'); row.append(avatarEl(null), profileLink(member.username, '@' + member.username + (member.is_owner ? ' Â· owner' : member.is_admin ? ' Â· admin' : '')));
        if (group.is_owner && !member.is_owner) {
          const adminButton = el('button', member.is_admin ? 'Remove admin' : 'Make admin'); adminButton.type = 'button';
          adminButton.onclick = async () => { const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/members/${member.id}/admin`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ is_admin: !member.is_admin }) }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel); else alert('Could not update the community admin role.'); };
          row.append(adminButton);
        }
        if ((group.is_owner || group.is_admin) && !member.is_owner && !member.is_admin) {
          const statusButton = el('button', member.enabled ? 'Pause access' : 'Enable access'); statusButton.type = 'button';
          statusButton.onclick = async () => { const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/members/${member.id}/status`, { method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ enabled: !member.enabled }) }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel); else alert('Could not update member access.'); };
          const remove = el('button', 'Remove'); remove.type = 'button'; remove.onclick = async () => { if (!window.confirm(`Remove @${member.username} from this community?`)) return; const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/members/${member.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityMembers(id, group, panel); else alert('Could not remove that member.'); };
          row.append(statusButton, remove);
        } else if (!member.enabled) row.append(el('span', 'Access paused', 'community-meta'));
        section.append(row);
      });
      panel.replaceChildren(section);
    }

    async function loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel) {
      panel.replaceChildren();
      const composer = el('section', undefined, 'community-composer'); composer.append(el('h2', 'Start a discussion'));
      const form = el('form', undefined, 'community-discussion-form');
      const title = el('input'); title.placeholder = 'Give your topic a clear title'; title.maxLength = 120; title.required = true;
      const body = el('textarea'); body.placeholder = 'Ask a question, share advice, or start a conversationâ€¦'; body.maxLength = 5000; body.required = true;
      const submit = el('button', 'Share with community'); submit.type = 'submit'; const status = el('p', '', 'community-note'); form.append(title, body, submit, status); composer.append(form); panel.append(composer);
      form.onsubmit = async event => { event.preventDefault(); submit.disabled = true; const result = await post(`/api/communities/${id}/posts`, { title: title.value, body: body.value }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel); else status.textContent = 'Could not post. Check your title and message.'; submit.disabled = false; };
      const response = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/posts`);
      if (!response.ok) { panel.append(el('p', 'Discussions could not be loaded.', 'empty')); return; }
      const posts = await response.json(); const postsBox = el('section', undefined, 'community-posts');
      if (!posts.length) postsBox.append(el('p', 'No discussions yet. Be the first to start a conversation.', 'empty'));
      posts.forEach(topic => {
        const card = el('article', undefined, 'community-post');
        const postHead = el('header', undefined, 'community-post-head');
        const author = el('div', undefined, 'community-post-author'); author.append(profileLink(topic.username, '@' + topic.username), el('span', new Date(topic.created_at).toLocaleString(), 'community-meta'));
        postHead.append(avatarEl(topic.avatar_url), author); card.append(postHead);
        const content = el('div', undefined, 'community-post-content'); content.append(el('h3', topic.title), el('p', topic.body)); card.append(content);
        const actions = el('div', undefined, 'community-post-actions');
        const repliesButton = el('button', `${topic.comment_count} replies`); repliesButton.type = 'button';
        const replies = el('div', undefined, 'community-comments'); replies.hidden = true;
        repliesButton.onclick = async () => { replies.hidden = !replies.hidden; if (!replies.hidden && !replies.dataset.loaded) { const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/posts/${topic.id}/comments`); if (result.ok) { const rows = await result.json(); rows.forEach(comment => { const row = el('div', undefined, 'community-comment'); row.append(el('span', '@' + comment.username + ': ' + comment.body)); if (comment.can_delete) { const del = el('button', 'Delete reply'); del.type = 'button'; del.onclick = async () => { if (!window.confirm('Delete this reply?')) return; const response = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/posts/${topic.id}/comments/${comment.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); if (response.ok) loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel); }; row.append(del); } replies.append(row); }); replies.dataset.loaded = '1'; } } };
        actions.append(repliesButton);
        if (topic.can_delete) { const del = el('button', 'Delete discussion'); del.type = 'button'; del.onclick = async () => { if (!window.confirm('Delete this discussion and its replies?')) return; const result = await fetch(`/api/communities/${id}/posts/${topic.id}`, { method: 'DELETE' }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel); }; actions.append(del); }
        card.append(actions, replies);
        const replyForm = el('form', undefined, 'community-toolbar'); const input = el('input'); input.placeholder = 'Write a reply'; input.maxLength = 2000; input.required = true; const send = el('button', 'Reply'); send.type = 'submit'; replyForm.append(input, send);
        replyForm.onsubmit = async event => { event.preventDefault(); send.disabled = true; const result = await post(`/api/communities/${id}/posts/${topic.id}/comments`, { body: input.value }); if (result.ok) loadCommunityDiscussion(id, group, panel); else { alert('Could not add your reply.'); send.disabled = false; } };
        card.append(replyForm); postsBox.append(card);
      });
      panel.append(postsBox);
    }

    function showHome() {
      stopInboxPolling();
      document.getElementById('homeView').hidden = false;
      document.getElementById('profileView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('inboxView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('userSearchView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('settingsView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('signupView').hidden = true;
      renderTabs();
      loadFeed();
      loadActivity();
    }

    function showSignup() {
      stopInboxPolling();
      ['homeView', 'profileView', 'inboxView', 'userSearchView', 'settingsView'].forEach(id => {
        document.getElementById(id).hidden = true;
      });
      const view = document.getElementById('signupView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.replaceChildren();
      const back = el('a', 'â† Back to Community', 'signup-back');
      back.href = '#/';
      const card = el('section', undefined, 'signup-card');
      card.append(
        el('h1', 'Create your account'),
        el('p', 'Join Community and start connecting with your community.'),
      );
      const form = el('form', undefined, 'signup-form');
      const addField = (labelText, name, type, options = {}) => {
        const label = el('label', labelText, 'signup-field' + (options.full ? ' full' : ''));
        const input = type === 'textarea' ? el('textarea') : el('input');
        input.name = name;
        if (type !== 'textarea') input.type = type;
        if (options.required) input.required = true;
        if (options.minLength) input.minLength = options.minLength;
        if (options.maxLength) input.maxLength = options.maxLength;
        if (options.min !== undefined) input.min = options.min;
        if (options.max !== undefined) input.max = options.max;
        if (options.autocomplete) input.autocomplete = options.autocomplete;
        if (options.placeholder) input.placeholder = options.placeholder;
        label.append(input);
        form.append(label);
      };
      addField('First name', 'first_name', 'text', { required: true, maxLength: 50, autocomplete: 'given-name' });
      addField('Last name', 'last_name', 'text', { required: true, maxLength: 50, autocomplete: 'family-name' });
      addField('Email address', 'email', 'email', { required: true, maxLength: 254, autocomplete: 'email' });
      addField('Username', 'username', 'text', { required: true, minLength: 3, maxLength: 30, autocomplete: 'username' });
      addField('Age', 'age', 'number', { required: true, min: 13, max: 120, autocomplete: 'off' });
      addField('Phone number (optional)', 'phone_number', 'tel', { maxLength: 30, autocomplete: 'tel' });
      addField('Bio (optional)', 'bio', 'textarea', { maxLength: 300, full: true });
      addField('Password (8 characters minimum)', 'password', 'password', { required: true, minLength: 8, maxLength: 128, autocomplete: 'new-password', full: true });
      const submit = el('button', 'Create account', 'signup-submit');
      submit.type = 'submit';
      const message = el('p', undefined, 'signup-message');
      message.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      form.append(submit, message);
      form.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        submit.disabled = true;
        message.textContent = '';
        const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form).entries());
        values.age = Number(values.age);
        try {
          const response = await post('/api/signup', values);
          if (response.status === 409) {
            message.textContent = 'That username or email is already registered.';
          } else if (response.status === 403) {
            message.textContent = 'New account signups are currently closed by an administrator.';
          } else if (response.status === 400) {
            message.textContent = 'Check your email, name, username, age, phone number, bio and password.';
          } else if (!response.ok) {
            message.textContent = 'Could not create your account. Please try again.';
          } else {
            location.hash = '#/';
            await renderAuth();
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not create account:', error);
          message.textContent = 'Could not create your account. Check your connection and try again.';
        } finally {
          submit.disabled = false;
        }
      };
      card.append(form);
      view.append(back, card);
    }

    function renderProfileDashboard(username) {
      const dashboardCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card dashboard-card');
      dashboardCard.id = 'profileDashboard';
      dashboardCard.append(
        el('h2', 'Your dashboard'),
        el('p', 'A quick look at your profile activity and connections. New followers are counted over the last 30 days.'),
      );
      const status = el('p', 'Loading your dashboard...', 'settings-status');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      dashboardCard.append(status);

      (async () => {
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/dashboard');
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Dashboard request failed: ' + response.status);
          const dashboard = await response.json();
          const stats = dashboard.stats;
          const profilePath = '#/u/' + encodeURIComponent(username);
          const metrics = el('div', undefined, 'dashboard-metrics');
          [
            ['profile_visits_count', 'Profile visits', profilePath + '#dashboard-visitors'],
            ['follower_count', 'Followers', profilePath + '#dashboard-followers'],
            ['new_followers_count', 'New followers Â· 30 days', profilePath + '#dashboard-new-followers'],
            ['following_count', 'Following', profilePath + '#dashboard-following'],
            ['posts_shared_count', 'Posts published', profilePath + '#dashboard-posts'],
            ['posts_reshared_by_others_count', 'Your posts reshared by others', profilePath + '#dashboard-posts'],
            ['pending_personal_requests_count', 'Pending personal requests', '#/inbox'],
            ['admirers_count', 'Admirers', profilePath + '#dashboard-admirers'],
            ['blocked_profiles_count', 'Blocked profiles', profilePath + '#dashboard-blocked-profiles'],
          ].forEach(([key, label, destination]) => {
            const metric = el('a', undefined, 'dashboard-metric');
            metric.href = destination;
            metric.append(
              el('strong', String(stats[key])),
              el('span', label),
              el('span', 'View details â†’', 'dashboard-metric-action'),
            );
            metrics.append(metric);
          });
          const lists = el('div', undefined, 'dashboard-lists');
          const addDashboardList = (title, people, emptyText, timestampKey, sectionId) => {
            const section = el('section', undefined, 'dashboard-list');
            section.id = sectionId;
            section.append(el('h3', title));
            const list = el('div', undefined, 'activity-list');
            if (people.length === 0) {
              list.append(el('p', emptyText, 'activity-empty'));
            } else {
              people.forEach(person => {
                const link = profileLink(person.username, '');
                link.className = 'dashboard-user';
                link.append(avatarEl(person.avatar_url), el('span', '@' + person.username));
                if (timestampKey && person[timestampKey]) {
                  link.append(el('span', timeAgo(person[timestampKey]), 'activity-time'));
                }
                list.append(link);
              });
            }
            section.append(list);
            lists.append(section);
          };
          addDashboardList(
            'Recent profile visitors',
            dashboard.visitors,
            'No recent profile visits, or visitor history is turned off.',
            'last_visited_at',
            'dashboard-visitors',
          );
          addDashboardList(
            'New followers Â· last 30 days',
            dashboard.new_followers,
            'No new followers in the last 30 days.',
            null,
            'dashboard-new-followers',
          );
          addDashboardList(
            'Blocked profiles',
            dashboard.blocked_profiles,
            'You have not blocked anyone.',
            null,
            'dashboard-blocked-profiles',
          );
          dashboardCard.replaceChildren(
            el('h2', 'Your dashboard'),
            el('p', 'A quick look at your profile activity and connections. New followers are counted over the last 30 days.'),
            metrics,
            lists,
          );
          dashboardCard.dispatchEvent(new Event('dashboard-ready'));
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not load profile dashboard:', error);
          status.textContent = 'Your dashboard could not be loaded. Refresh the page and try again.';
          status.classList.add('dashboard-error');
          dashboardCard.dispatchEvent(new Event('dashboard-ready'));
        }
      })();
      return dashboardCard;
    }

    async function showSettings() {
      stopInboxPolling();
      document.getElementById('homeView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('profileView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('inboxView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('userSearchView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('signupView').hidden = true;
      const view = document.getElementById('settingsView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.replaceChildren();
      const heading = el('header', undefined, 'settings-heading');
      heading.append(
        el('h1', 'Settings'),
        el('p', 'Choose who can find you, how people can contact you, and what activity you keep.'),
      );
      view.append(heading);
      if (!loggedIn) {
        const card = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card');
        card.append(el('p', 'Log in to manage your account settings.'));
        const home = el('a', 'Back to Community');
        home.href = '#/';
        card.append(home);
        view.append(card);
        return;
      }

      if (currentIsAdmin) {
        const adminCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card settings-admin-card');
        adminCard.append(
          el('h2', currentIsSuperadmin ? 'Superadmin controls' : 'Admin controls'),
          el('p', currentIsSuperadmin
            ? 'Open the dashboard to manage the app, administrators, accounts, chats, and community activity.'
            : 'Open the dashboard to review app activity and account settings.'),
        );
        const adminLink = el('a', currentIsSuperadmin ? 'Open superadmin dashboard' : 'Open admin hub', 'settings-admin-link');
        adminLink.href = '#/admin';
        adminCard.append(adminLink);
        view.append(adminCard);
      }

      let settings;
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/me/settings');
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Settings request failed: ' + response.status);
        settings = await response.json();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not load settings:', error);
        view.append(el('p', 'Settings could not be loaded. Refresh the page and try again.', 'settings-status'));
        return;
      }

      const grid = el('div', undefined, 'settings-grid');
      const form = el('form', undefined, 'settings-stack');
      const privacyCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card');
      privacyCard.append(
        el('h2', 'Privacy & visibility'),
        el('p', 'Control how your Community profile and availability appear to other people.'),
      );
      const addSelect = (parent, name, labelText, helpText, choices) => {
        const row = el('div', undefined, 'setting-row');
        row.classList.add('setting-select-row');
        const copy = el('div', undefined, 'setting-copy');
        copy.append(el('strong', labelText), el('span', helpText));
        const select = el('select');
        select.name = name;
        select.setAttribute('aria-label', labelText);
        choices.forEach(([value, label]) => {
          const option = el('option', label);
          option.value = value;
          select.append(option);
        });
        select.value = settings[name];
        row.append(copy, select);
        parent.append(row);
      };
      const addToggle = (parent, name, labelText, helpText) => {
        const row = el('label', undefined, 'setting-row');
        const copy = el('span', undefined, 'setting-copy');
        copy.append(el('strong', labelText), el('span', helpText));
        const input = el('input');
        input.type = 'checkbox';
        input.name = name;
        input.checked = settings[name];
        row.append(copy, input);
        parent.append(row);
      };

      addSelect(privacyCard, 'profile_visibility', 'Who can see my profile', 'Private profiles and their posts are only visible to approved followers.', [
        ['public', 'Everyone'],
        ['followers', 'Followers only'],
        ['private', 'Only me'],
      ]);
      addSelect(privacyCard, 'message_privacy', 'Who can message me', 'Personal chat requests follow the same rule.', [
        ['everyone', 'Everyone'],
        ['following', 'People I follow'],
        ['nobody', 'Nobody'],
      ]);
      addToggle(privacyCard, 'show_activity_status', 'Show when I am online', 'Turn this off to disappear from the online people list.');
      form.append(privacyCard);

      const notificationCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card');
      notificationCard.append(
        el('h2', 'Notifications & activity'),
        el('p', 'Manage in-app mentions and profile visit history. Email and push notifications are not enabled in this app.'),
      );
      addToggle(notificationCard, 'notify_mentions', 'Mentions & photo tags', 'Show mention and tag alerts in your activity panel.');
      addToggle(notificationCard, 'keep_profile_visitors', 'Keep profile visitor history', 'Record recent visits to your profile and show them in your activity panel.');
      form.append(notificationCard);

      const accountCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card settings-danger');
      accountCard.append(
        el('h2', 'Account status'),
        el('p', 'Pausing hides your profile from discovery and stops new posts. You can still sign in here and re-enable it anytime.'),
      );
      addToggle(accountCard, 'account_enabled', 'Keep my account enabled', 'Turn off to pause your account; existing content is hidden from other people.');
      form.append(accountCard);

      const saveRow = el('div', undefined, 'settings-save');
      const save = el('button', 'Save settings');
      save.type = 'submit';
      const status = el('span', undefined, 'settings-status');
      status.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      saveRow.append(save, status);
      form.append(saveRow);
      form.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        save.disabled = true;
        status.textContent = 'Saving...';
        const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form).entries());
        const payload = {
          profile_visibility: values.profile_visibility,
          message_privacy: values.message_privacy,
          show_activity_status: form.elements.show_activity_status.checked,
          keep_profile_visitors: form.elements.keep_profile_visitors.checked,
          notify_mentions: form.elements.notify_mentions.checked,
          account_enabled: form.elements.account_enabled.checked,
        };
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/settings', {
            method: 'PUT',
            headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
            body: JSON.stringify(payload),
          });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Settings update failed: ' + response.status);
          settings = await response.json();
          status.textContent = 'Settings saved.';
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not save settings:', error);
          status.textContent = 'Could not save settings. Please try again.';
        } finally {
          save.disabled = false;
        }
      };

      const blockedCard = el('section', undefined, 'settings-card');
      blockedCard.append(
        el('h2', 'Blocked accounts'),
        el('p', 'Blocked people cannot view your profile, message you, or interact with you. Blocking also removes your existing follows between each other.'),
      );
      const blockForm = el('form', undefined, 'block-form');
      const blockInput = el('input');
      blockInput.type = 'text';
      blockInput.name = 'username';
      blockInput.maxLength = 30;
      blockInput.autocomplete = 'off';
      blockInput.placeholder = 'Username to block';
      blockInput.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Username to block');
      const blockButton = el('button', 'Block');
      blockButton.type = 'submit';
      blockForm.append(blockInput, blockButton);
      const blockStatus = el('span', undefined, 'settings-status');
      blockStatus.setAttribute('role', 'status');
      const blockedList = el('div', undefined, 'blocked-list');
      blockedCard.append(blockForm, blockStatus, blockedList);
      blockForm.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const username = blockInput.value.trim();
        if (!username) return;
        blockButton.disabled = true;
        blockStatus.textContent = 'Blocking...';
        try {
          const response = await post('/api/me/blocked-users', { username });
          if (!response.ok) {
            blockStatus.textContent = response.status === 404
              ? 'No account found with that username.'
              : response.status === 400
                ? 'Enter a valid username other than your own.'
                : 'Could not block that account. Please try again.';
            return;
          }
          blockInput.value = '';
          blockStatus.textContent = 'Account blocked.';
          await loadBlockedUsers();
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not block account:', error);
          blockStatus.textContent = 'Could not block that account. Check your connection and try again.';
        } finally {
          blockButton.disabled = false;
        }
      };

      async function loadBlockedUsers() {
        blockedList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading blocked accounts...', 'activity-empty'));
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/blocked-users');
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Blocked accounts request failed: ' + response.status);
          const users = await response.json();
          blockedList.textContent = '';
          if (users.length === 0) {
            blockedList.append(el('p', 'You have not blocked anyone.', 'activity-empty'));
            return;
          }
          users.forEach(user => {
            const row = el('div', undefined, 'blocked-user');
            row.append(avatarEl(user.avatar_url), el('span', '@' + user.username, 'ulink'));
            const unblock = el('button', 'Unblock');
            unblock.type = 'button';
            unblock.onclick = async () => {
              unblock.disabled = true;
              try {
                const result = await fetch('/api/me/blocked-users/' + encodeURIComponent(user.username), { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (!result.ok) throw new Error('Unblock request failed: ' + result.status);
                await loadBlockedUsers();
              } catch (error) {
                console.error('Could not unblock account:', error);
                blockStatus.textContent = 'Could not unblock that account. Please try again.';
                unblock.disabled = false;
              }
            };
            row.append(unblock);
            blockedList.append(row);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not load blocked accounts:', error);
          blockedList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Blocked accounts could not be loaded. Please try again.', 'connection-error'));
        }
      }
      await loadBlockedUsers();
      grid.append(form, blockedCard);
      view.append(grid);
    }

    async function showUserSearch() {
      stopInboxPolling();
      document.getElementById('homeView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('profileView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('inboxView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('settingsView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('signupView').hidden = true;
      const view = document.getElementById('userSearchView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.replaceChildren();

      const heading = el('header', undefined, 'user-search-heading');
      heading.append(
        el('h1', 'Find people'),
        el('p', 'Search by a personâ€™s name or username and open their profile.'),
      );
      view.append(heading);

      const results = el('div', undefined, 'user-search-results');
      results.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      if (!loggedIn) {
        view.append(
          el('p', 'Log in to search for people.', 'user-search-message'),
          results,
        );
        return;
      }

      const form = el('form', undefined, 'user-search-form');
      const input = el('input');
      input.type = 'search';
      input.name = 'q';
      input.maxLength = 50;
      input.autocomplete = 'off';
      input.placeholder = 'Search by name or username';
      input.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Search by name or username');
      const submit = el('button', 'Search');
      submit.type = 'submit';
      const status = el('p', 'Enter at least 2 letters to search.', 'user-search-message');
      form.append(input, submit);
      view.append(form, status, results);
      form.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const query = input.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 2) {
          status.textContent = 'Enter at least 2 letters to search.';
          results.replaceChildren();
          return;
        }
        submit.disabled = true;
        status.textContent = 'Searching...';
        results.replaceChildren();
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/users/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
          if (response.status === 401) {
            status.textContent = 'Please log in again to search.';
            return;
          }
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('User search request failed: ' + response.status);
          const users = await response.json();
          if (users.length === 0) {
            status.textContent = 'No matching users found.';
            return;
          }
          status.textContent = users.length === 50
            ? 'Showing the first 50 matching users.'
            : users.length + (users.length === 1 ? ' person found.' : ' people found.');
          users.forEach(user => {
            const link = profileLink(user.username, '');
            link.className = 'user-search-result';
            const label = el('span', undefined, 'user-search-result-label');
            if (user.display_name) label.append(el('strong', user.display_name));
            label.append(el('span', '@' + user.username));
            link.append(avatarEl(user.avatar_url), label);
            results.append(link);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not search users:', error);
          status.textContent = 'Could not search users. Please try again.';
        } finally {
          submit.disabled = false;
        }
      };
      input.focus();
    }

    function setupSidebarPeopleSearch() {
      const form = document.getElementById('sidebarPeopleSearchForm');
      const input = document.getElementById('sidebarPeopleSearchInput');
      const status = document.getElementById('sidebarPeopleSearchStatus');
      const results = document.getElementById('sidebarPeopleSearchResults');
      let requestId = 0;
      form.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const query = input.value.trim();
        const currentRequest = ++requestId;
        if (query.length < 2) {
          status.textContent = 'Enter at least 2 letters to search.';
          results.replaceChildren();
          return;
        }
        status.textContent = 'Searching...';
        results.replaceChildren();
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/users/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
          if (currentRequest !== requestId) return;
          if (response.status === 401) {
            status.textContent = 'Log in to search for people.';
            return;
          }
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('People search failed.');
          const users = await response.json();
          if (!users.length) {
            status.textContent = 'No matching people found.';
            return;
          }
          status.textContent = users.length + (users.length === 1 ? ' person found.' : ' people found.');
          users.forEach(user => {
            const link = profileLink(user.username, '');
            link.className = 'user-search-result';
            const label = el('span', undefined, 'user-search-result-label');
            if (user.display_name) label.append(el('strong', user.display_name));
            label.append(el('span', '@' + user.username));
            link.append(avatarEl(user.avatar_url), label);
            results.append(link);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          if (currentRequest !== requestId) return;
          console.error('Could not search people:', error);
          status.textContent = 'Could not search. Please try again.';
        }
      };
    }

    function renderTabs() {
      const box = document.getElementById('tabs');
      box.textContent = '';
      if (!loggedIn) { scope = 'all'; return; }
      [['all', 'Everyone'], ['following', 'Following']].forEach(([key, label]) => {
        const b = el('button', label, 'tab' + (scope === key ? ' active' : ''));
        b.onclick = () => { scope = key; renderTabs(); loadFeed(); };
        box.append(b);
      });
    }

    // ---------- edit-profile form (only shown on your own profile) ----------
    function fieldBox(labelText, input) {
      const wrap = el('div');
      wrap.append(el('label', labelText), input);
      return wrap;
    }

    function editForm(pr) {
      const f = el('div', undefined, 'editform');
      f.append(el('div', 'Everything below is optional. It appears on your profile according to your visibility settings.', 'note'));

      // profile picture
      const file = el('input');
      file.type = 'file';
      file.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif';
      const upload = el('button', 'Upload picture');
      const remove = el('button', 'Remove picture');
      const avMsg = el('span', undefined, 'hint');
      upload.onclick = async () => {
        if (!file.files[0]) { avMsg.textContent = 'Choose a picture first'; return; }
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('image', file.files[0]);
        avMsg.textContent = 'Uploading...';
        const r = await fetch('/api/me/avatar', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
        if (r.ok) showProfile(pr.username, true);
        else if (r.status === 413) avMsg.textContent = 'Picture too large (max 5 MB)';
        else if (r.status === 415) avMsg.textContent = 'Only JPEG, PNG, WebP or GIF pictures';
        else if (r.status === 401) avMsg.textContent = 'Please log in again';
        else avMsg.textContent = 'Something went wrong';
      };
      remove.onclick = async () => {
        const r = await fetch('/api/me/avatar', { method: 'DELETE' });
        if (r.ok) showProfile(pr.username, true);
      };
      const avRow = el('div', undefined, 'row');
      avRow.append(file, upload, remove, avMsg);
      f.append(fieldBox('Profile picture (JPEG, PNG, WebP or GIF, max 5 MB)', avRow));

      // text fields
      const dn = el('input'); dn.type = 'text'; dn.maxLength = 50; dn.value = pr.display_name || '';
      const bioEditor = el('div', undefined, 'rich-editor');
      bioEditor.contentEditable = 'true'; bioEditor.setAttribute('role', 'textbox'); bioEditor.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Profile bio'); bioEditor.setAttribute('aria-multiline', 'true'); bioEditor.dataset.placeholder = 'Tell people what you care aboutâ€¦'; bioEditor.dataset.maxLength = '500';
      const initialBio = safeRichBio(pr.bio);
      bioEditor.append(...Array.from(initialBio.childNodes));
      const bioToolbar = el('div', undefined, 'rich-editor-toolbar');
      [['B', 'bold', 'Bold'], ['I', 'italic', 'Italic'], ['U', 'underline', 'Underline'], ['â€¢', 'insertUnorderedList', 'Bulleted list'], ['1.', 'insertOrderedList', 'Numbered list'], ['â', 'formatBlock', 'Quote']].forEach(([label, command, title]) => { const tool = el('button', label); tool.type = 'button'; tool.title = title; tool.setAttribute('aria-label', title); tool.onclick = () => { bioEditor.focus(); document.execCommand(command, false, command === 'formatBlock' ? 'blockquote' : null); }; bioToolbar.append(tool); });
      const bioWrap = el('div', undefined, 'rich-editor-wrap'); bioWrap.append(bioToolbar, bioEditor);
      const bioStatus = el('p', 'Use the toolbar to format your bio. Keep it under 500 characters.', 'note');
      bioEditor.addEventListener('input', () => { const count = bioEditor.innerText.trim().length; bioStatus.textContent = `${count}/500 characters`; bioStatus.classList.toggle('dashboard-error', count > 500); });
      const age = el('input'); age.type = 'number'; age.min = 13; age.max = 120;
      age.value = pr.age == null ? '' : pr.age;
      const loc = el('input'); loc.type = 'text'; loc.maxLength = 60; loc.value = pr.location || '';
      loc.placeholder = 'Start typing a city, region or country';
      loc.autocomplete = 'off';
      const locationWrap = el('div', undefined, 'location-search-wrap');
      const locationResults = el('div', undefined, 'location-search-results');
      locationResults.hidden = true;
      locationWrap.append(loc, locationResults);
      let locationSearchTimer = null;
      let locationSearchId = 0;
      loc.addEventListener('input', () => {
        clearTimeout(locationSearchTimer);
        const term = loc.value.trim();
        if (term.length < 2) { locationResults.hidden = true; locationResults.replaceChildren(); return; }
        locationSearchTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
          const requestId = ++locationSearchId;
          locationResults.hidden = false;
          locationResults.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Searching locationsâ€¦', 'note'));
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/locations/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(term));
            if (requestId !== locationSearchId) return;
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Location search is temporarily unavailable.');
            const places = await response.json();
            locationResults.replaceChildren();
            if (!places.length) locationResults.append(el('p', 'No matching places. You can keep typing your own location.', 'note'));
            places.forEach(place => {
              const option = el('button', place.label); option.type = 'button';
              option.onclick = () => { loc.value = place.label; locationResults.hidden = true; locationResults.replaceChildren(); };
              locationResults.append(option);
            });
          } catch (error) {
            if (requestId === locationSearchId) locationResults.replaceChildren(el('p', error.message, 'note'));
          }
        }, 600);
      });
      const dob = el('input'); dob.type = 'date'; dob.value = pr.date_of_birth || '';
      const maxDob = new Date(); maxDob.setFullYear(maxDob.getFullYear() - 13);
      dob.max = maxDob.toISOString().slice(0, 10); dob.min = '1900-01-01';
      const company = el('input'); company.type = 'text'; company.maxLength = 100; company.placeholder = 'Company or organization'; company.value = pr.company_name || '';
      const designation = el('input'); designation.type = 'text'; designation.maxLength = 100; designation.placeholder = 'Role or title'; designation.value = pr.designation || '';
      const contactEmail = el('input'); contactEmail.type = 'email'; contactEmail.maxLength = 254; contactEmail.autocomplete = 'email'; contactEmail.value = pr.contact_email || '';
      const phone = el('input'); phone.type = 'tel'; phone.maxLength = 30; phone.autocomplete = 'tel'; phone.value = pr.phone_number || '';
      const instagram = el('input'); instagram.type = 'text'; instagram.maxLength = 60; instagram.placeholder = '@username'; instagram.value = pr.instagram_handle || '';
      const facebook = el('input'); facebook.type = 'text'; facebook.maxLength = 60; facebook.placeholder = 'profile name'; facebook.value = pr.facebook_handle || '';
      const tiktok = el('input'); tiktok.type = 'text'; tiktok.maxLength = 60; tiktok.placeholder = '@username'; tiktok.value = pr.tiktok_handle || '';
