      const linkedin = el('input'); linkedin.type = 'text'; linkedin.maxLength = 100; linkedin.placeholder = 'profile slug'; linkedin.value = pr.linkedin_handle || '';
      const visibility = value => {
        const select = el('select');
        [['everyone', 'Everyone'], ['friends', 'Friends (mutual follows)'], ['self', 'Only me']].forEach(([key, label]) => {
          const option = el('option', label); option.value = key; select.append(option);
        });
        select.value = value || 'self';
        return select;
      };
      const birthdayVisibility = visibility(pr.birthday_visibility);
      const workVisibility = visibility(pr.work_visibility);
      const emailVisibility = visibility(pr.email_visibility);
      const phoneVisibility = visibility(pr.phone_visibility);
      const socialVisibility = visibility(pr.social_visibility);
      const mood = el('select');
      [
        ['', 'Prefer not to say'],
        ['Bored', 'Bored'],
        ['Romantic', 'Romantic'],
        ['Angry', 'Angry'],
        ['Sad', 'Sad'],
        ['Happy', 'Happy'],
        ['Calm', 'Calm'],
        ['Excited', 'Excited'],
        ['Playful', 'Playful'],
        ['Lonely', 'Lonely'],
        ['Grateful', 'Grateful'],
        ['Stressed', 'Stressed'],
      ].forEach(([value, label]) => {
        const option = el('option', label);
        option.value = value;
        mood.append(option);
      });
      mood.value = pr.mood || '';
      const ints = el('input'); ints.type = 'text'; ints.maxLength = 200;
      ints.placeholder = 'e.g. hiking, photography, chess';
      ints.value = pr.interests || '';
      const rel = el('select');
      ['', 'Single', 'In a relationship', 'Engaged', 'Married', "It's complicated"].forEach(v => {
        const o = el('option', v === '' ? 'Prefer not to say' : v);
        o.value = v;
        rel.append(o);
      });
      rel.value = pr.relationship_status || '';

      f.append(
        fieldBox('Display name (max 50 characters)', dn),
        fieldBox('About you Â· rich text', bioWrap),
        bioStatus,
        fieldBox('Age (13-120)', age),
        fieldBox('Location (search worldwide or type your own)', locationWrap),
        el('p', 'Location search powered by OpenStreetMap data via Photon.', 'note'),
        fieldBox('Birthday (kept private by default)', dob),
        fieldBox('Birthday visibility', birthdayVisibility),
        fieldBox('Company', company),
        fieldBox('Designation', designation),
        fieldBox('Work visibility', workVisibility),
        fieldBox('Contact email', contactEmail),
        fieldBox('Email visibility', emailVisibility),
        fieldBox('Phone number', phone),
        fieldBox('Phone visibility', phoneVisibility),
        fieldBox('Instagram handle', instagram),
        fieldBox('Facebook handle', facebook),
        fieldBox('TikTok handle', tiktok),
        fieldBox('LinkedIn profile name', linkedin),
        fieldBox('Social handles visibility', socialVisibility),
        fieldBox('Current mood (optional)', mood),
        fieldBox('Interests (separate with commas)', ints),
        fieldBox('Relationship status', rel),
      );

      const save = el('button', 'Save profile');
      const msg = el('span', undefined, 'hint');
      save.onclick = async () => {
        if (bioEditor.innerText.trim().length > 500) { msg.textContent = 'Keep your bio under 500 characters.'; return; }
        const body = {
          display_name: dn.value,
          bio: bioEditor.innerHTML,
          age: age.value === '' ? null : parseInt(age.value, 10),
          date_of_birth: dob.value || null,
          birthday_visibility: birthdayVisibility.value,
          location: loc.value,
          company_name: company.value,
          designation: designation.value,
          work_visibility: workVisibility.value,
          contact_email: contactEmail.value,
          email_visibility: emailVisibility.value,
          phone_number: phone.value,
          phone_visibility: phoneVisibility.value,
          instagram_handle: instagram.value.replace(/^@/, ''),
          facebook_handle: facebook.value.replace(/^@/, ''),
          tiktok_handle: tiktok.value.replace(/^@/, ''),
          linkedin_handle: linkedin.value.replace(/^@/, ''),
          social_visibility: socialVisibility.value,
          mood: mood.value,
          interests: ints.value,
          relationship_status: rel.value,
        };
        const r = await fetch('/api/me/profile', {
          method: 'PUT',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(body),
        });
        if (r.ok) showProfile(pr.username);
        else if (r.status === 400) msg.textContent = 'Please check your birthday, email, phone, social handles and text lengths.';
        else if (r.status === 401) msg.textContent = 'Please log in again';
        else msg.textContent = 'Something went wrong';
      };
      const saveRow = el('div', undefined, 'row');
      saveRow.append(save, msg);
      f.append(saveRow);
      return f;
    }

    // ---------- profile page ----------
    async function showProfile(name, editing, dashboardSection = null) {
      stopInboxPolling();
      document.getElementById('homeView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('inboxView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('userSearchView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('settingsView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('signupView').hidden = true;
      const view = document.getElementById('profileView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.textContent = '';

      const back = el('a', 'â† Back to feed', 'back');
      back.href = '#/';
      view.append(back);

      const res = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(name));
      if (!res.ok) { view.append(el('p', 'User not found', 'empty')); return; }
      const pr = await res.json();

      const profileHero = el('section', undefined, 'profile-hero');
      const profileCover = el('div', undefined, 'profile-cover');
      if (pr.cover_image_url) { const cover = el('img'); cover.src = pr.cover_image_url; cover.alt = ''; profileCover.append(cover); }
      const profileHeroBody = el('div', undefined, 'profile-hero-body');
      const head = el('div', undefined, 'phead');
      head.append(avatarWithMood(pr.avatar_url, pr.mood, true));
      const info = el('div');
      const usernameLine = el('div', undefined, 'profile-username-line');
      usernameLine.append(el('span', '@' + pr.username, pr.display_name ? 'psub' : 'pname'));
      if (loggedIn && !pr.is_me) {
        const actions = el('details', undefined, 'profile-actions');
        const summary = el('summary', 'Actions â–¾');
        summary.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Actions for @' + pr.username);
        const menu = el('div', undefined, 'profile-actions-menu');
        const viewProfile = el('a', 'View profile');
        viewProfile.setAttribute('aria-label', 'View profile for @' + pr.username);
        viewProfile.href = '#/u/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username);
        viewProfile.onclick = () => { actions.open = false; };
        const messageButton = el('button', 'Send message');
        messageButton.type = 'button';
        messageButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Send message to @' + pr.username);
        const messageError = el('span', undefined, 'profile-message-error');
        messageButton.onclick = async () => {
          messageButton.disabled = true;
          messageError.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await post('/api/conversations', {
              usernames: [pr.username],
              title: '',
            });
            if (!response.ok) {
              messageError.textContent = response.status === 401
                ? 'Please log in again.'
                : 'Could not start a conversation.';
              return;
            }
            const conversation = await response.json();
            activeConversationId = conversation.id;
            if (location.hash === '#/inbox') {
              await showInbox();
            } else {
              location.hash = '#/inbox';
            }
          } catch {
            messageError.textContent = 'Could not start a conversation. Check your connection and try again.';
          } finally {
            messageButton.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        menu.append(viewProfile, messageButton, messageError);
        actions.append(summary, menu);
        const followButton = el('button', pr.followed_by_me ? 'Unfollow' : 'Follow', 'profile-follow-button');
        followButton.type = 'button';
        followButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(pr.followed_by_me));
        const followStatus = el('span', undefined, 'profile-follow-status');
        followStatus.setAttribute('role', 'status');
        followButton.onclick = async () => {
          followButton.disabled = true;
          followStatus.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await fetch(
              '/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username) + '/follow',
              { method: pr.followed_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST' },
            );
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Follow update failed: ' + response.status);
            await showProfile(pr.username);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not update profile follow:', error);
            followStatus.textContent = 'Could not update follow. Please try again.';
          } finally {
            followButton.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        usernameLine.append(actions, followButton, followStatus);
      }
      if (pr.display_name) {
        info.append(el('div', pr.display_name, 'pname'), usernameLine);
      } else {
        info.append(usernameLine);
      }

      if (pr.is_me) {
        const eb = el('button', editing ? 'Cancel' : 'Edit profile');
        eb.onclick = () => showProfile(pr.username, !editing);
        const pictureInput = el('input');
        pictureInput.type = 'file';
        pictureInput.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif';
        pictureInput.hidden = true;
        const pictureButton = el('button', pr.avatar_url ? 'Update profile picture' : 'Add profile picture');
        pictureButton.type = 'button';
        const pictureStatus = el('span', undefined, 'profile-feedback');
        pictureButton.onclick = () => pictureInput.click();
        pictureInput.onchange = async () => {
          if (!pictureInput.files || !pictureInput.files[0]) return;
          const form = new FormData();
          form.append('image', pictureInput.files[0]);
          pictureButton.disabled = true;
          pictureStatus.textContent = 'Uploading profile picture...';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/me/avatar', { method: 'POST', body: form });
            if (response.ok) {
              showProfile(pr.username, editing);
            } else if (response.status === 413) {
              pictureStatus.textContent = 'Picture too large (max 5 MB).';
            } else if (response.status === 415) {
              pictureStatus.textContent = 'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP or GIF picture.';
            } else if (response.status === 401) {
              pictureStatus.textContent = 'Please log in again.';
            } else {
              pictureStatus.textContent = 'Could not update your profile picture.';
            }
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not update profile picture:', error);
            pictureStatus.textContent = 'Could not update your picture. Check your connection and try again.';
          } finally {
            pictureInput.value = '';
            pictureButton.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        const pictureRow = el('div', undefined, 'row');
        pictureRow.append(eb, pictureButton, pictureInput, pictureStatus);
        info.append(pictureRow);
      } else if (!loggedIn) {
        info.append(el('span', 'Log in to follow', 'hint'));
      } else {
        if (pr.followed_by_me || pr.admired_by_me) {
          const admireButton = el('button', pr.admired_by_me ? 'â™¥ Admired' : 'â™¡ Admire');
          admireButton.type = 'button';
          admireButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(pr.admired_by_me));
          admireButton.onclick = async () => {
            admireButton.disabled = true;
            try {
              const response = await fetch(
                '/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username) + '/admire',
                { method: pr.admired_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST' },
              );
              if (response.ok) showProfile(pr.username);
              else alert(response.status === 403
                ? 'Follow this person before sending admiration.'
                : 'Could not update admiration. Please try again.');
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Could not update profile admiration:', error);
              alert('Could not update admiration. Check your connection and try again.');
            } finally {
              admireButton.disabled = false;
            }
          };
          info.append(admireButton);
        }
        const requestButton = el('button', 'Request personal chat');
        requestButton.type = 'button';
        const requestFeedback = el('span', undefined, 'profile-feedback');
        requestButton.onclick = async () => {
          requestButton.disabled = true;
          requestFeedback.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await post(
              '/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username) + '/personal-chat-requests',
            );
            if (response.ok) requestFeedback.textContent = 'Request sent. They can accept or decline it.';
            else requestFeedback.textContent = response.status === 404
              ? 'This profile is no longer available.'
              : 'Could not send the request. Please try again.';
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not send personal chat request:', error);
            requestFeedback.textContent = 'Could not send the request. Check your connection and try again.';
          } finally {
            requestButton.disabled = false;
          }
        };
        info.append(requestButton, requestFeedback);
      }
      head.append(info);
      profileHeroBody.append(head);
      profileHero.append(profileCover, profileHeroBody);
      view.append(profileHero);

      if (editing && pr.is_me) view.append(editForm(pr));

      const stats = el('div', undefined, 'stats');
      stats.append(
        el('span', pr.post_count + (pr.post_count === 1 ? ' post' : ' posts')),
      );
      const connectionsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'connections-panel');
      connectionsPanel.hidden = true;
      connectionsPanel.setAttribute('aria-live', 'polite');
      const connectionsHeading = el('h2');
      const connectionsList = el('div', undefined, 'connections-list');
      connectionsPanel.append(connectionsHeading, connectionsList);
      let openConnectionKind = null;
      async function showConnections(kind, count) {
        if (openConnectionKind === kind) {
          openConnectionKind = null;
          connectionsPanel.hidden = true;
          return;
        }
        openConnectionKind = kind;
        connectionsPanel.hidden = false;
        connectionsHeading.textContent = kind === 'followers'
          ? count + (count === 1 ? ' follower' : ' followers')
          : count + ' following';
        connectionsList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading...', 'connection-empty'));
        try {
          const response = await fetch(
            '/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username) + '/connections?kind=' + kind,
          );
          if (!response.ok) {
            throw new Error('Could not load ' + kind + ': HTTP ' + response.status);
          }
          const users = await response.json();
          if (openConnectionKind !== kind) return;
          connectionsHeading.textContent = kind === 'followers'
            ? users.length + (users.length === 1 ? ' follower' : ' followers')
            : users.length + ' following';
          connectionsList.textContent = '';
          if (users.length === 0) {
            connectionsList.append(el('p', 'No ' + kind + ' yet.', 'connection-empty'));
            return;
          }
          users.forEach(user => {
            const link = profileLink(user.username, '');
            link.className = 'connection-link';
            link.append(avatarEl(user.avatar_url), el('span', '@' + user.username));
            connectionsList.append(link);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not load profile connections:', error);
          if (openConnectionKind === kind) {
            connectionsList.replaceChildren(
              el('p', 'Could not load this list. Please try again.', 'connection-error'),
            );
          }
        }
      }
      const followersButton = el('button', pr.follower_count + (pr.follower_count === 1 ? ' follower' : ' followers'));
      followersButton.id = 'profileFollowers';
      followersButton.type = 'button';
      followersButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      followersButton.onclick = async () => {
        await showConnections('followers', pr.follower_count);
        followersButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(openConnectionKind === 'followers'));
        followingButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(openConnectionKind === 'following'));
      };
      const followingButton = el('button', pr.following_count + ' following');
      followingButton.id = 'profileFollowing';
      followingButton.type = 'button';
      followingButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', 'false');
      followingButton.onclick = async () => {
        await showConnections('following', pr.following_count);
        followingButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(openConnectionKind === 'following'));
        followersButton.setAttribute('aria-expanded', String(openConnectionKind === 'followers'));
      };
      stats.append(followersButton, followingButton);
      let admirationPanel;
      let admirationButton;
      if (pr.is_me && loggedIn) {
        admirationButton = el(
          'button',
          (pr.admiration_count || 0) + ((pr.admiration_count || 0) === 1 ? ' admirer' : ' admirers'),
        );
        admirationButton.id = 'profileAdmirers';
        admirationButton.type = 'button';
        admirationPanel = el('section', undefined, 'connections-panel');
        admirationPanel.hidden = true;
        const admirationHeading = el('h2', 'People who admire you');
        const admirationList = el('div', undefined, 'connections-list');
        admirationPanel.append(admirationHeading, admirationList);
        admirationButton.onclick = async () => {
          admirationPanel.hidden = !admirationPanel.hidden;
          if (admirationPanel.hidden) return;
          admirationList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading...', 'connection-empty'));
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(pr.username) + '/admirers');
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
            const admirers = await response.json();
            admirationList.textContent = '';
            if (admirers.length === 0) {
              admirationList.append(el('p', 'No admirers yet.', 'connection-empty'));
              return;
            }
            admirers.forEach(user => {
              const link = profileLink(user.username, '');
              link.className = 'connection-link';
              link.append(avatarEl(user.avatar_url), el('span', '@' + user.username));
              admirationList.append(link);
            });
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not load admirers:', error);
            admirationList.replaceChildren(
              el('p', 'Could not load this list. Please try again.', 'connection-error'),
            );
          }
        };
        stats.append(admirationButton);
      }
      view.append(stats);
      view.append(connectionsPanel);
      if (admirationPanel) view.append(admirationPanel);

      if (pr.is_me && loggedIn) {
        view.append(renderProfileDashboard(pr.username));
      }

      const profileInfo = el('section', undefined, 'profile-about-card profile-info-card');
      profileInfo.append(el('h2', 'About & contact'));
      const aboutSection = el('div', undefined, 'profile-info-section');
      let hasAboutDetails = false;
      if (pr.bio) { aboutSection.append(safeRichBio(pr.bio)); hasAboutDetails = true; }
      const details = el('div', undefined, 'details');
      if (pr.age != null) details.append(el('span', 'Age ' + pr.age));
      if (pr.location) details.append(el('span', 'ðŸ“ ' + pr.location));
      if (pr.relationship_status) details.append(el('span', 'ðŸ’¬ ' + pr.relationship_status));
      if (details.childNodes.length) { aboutSection.append(details); hasAboutDetails = true; }
      if (hasAboutDetails) profileInfo.append(aboutSection);

      const contactDetails = el('div', undefined, 'profile-contact profile-info-contact');
      let hasContactDetails = false;
      if (pr.date_of_birth) {
        const birthday = new Date(pr.date_of_birth + 'T00:00:00');
        contactDetails.append(el('span', 'ðŸŽ‚ Birthday Â· ' + birthday.toLocaleDateString(undefined, { month: 'long', day: 'numeric', year: 'numeric' })));
        hasContactDetails = true;
      }
      if (pr.company_name || pr.designation) {
        contactDetails.append(el('span', 'ðŸ’¼ ' + [pr.designation, pr.company_name].filter(Boolean).join(' at ')));
        hasContactDetails = true;
      }
      if (pr.contact_email) {
        const email = el('a', 'âœ‰ ' + pr.contact_email); email.href = 'mailto:' + pr.contact_email;
        contactDetails.append(email); hasContactDetails = true;
      }
      if (pr.phone_number) {
        const phone = el('a', 'â˜Ž ' + pr.phone_number); phone.href = 'tel:' + pr.phone_number.replace(/[^+\d]/g, '');
        contactDetails.append(phone); hasContactDetails = true;
      }
      const socialLinks = [
        ['Instagram', pr.instagram_handle, 'https://www.instagram.com/'],
        ['Facebook', pr.facebook_handle, 'https://www.facebook.com/'],
        ['TikTok', pr.tiktok_handle, 'https://www.tiktok.com/@'],
        ['LinkedIn', pr.linkedin_handle, 'https://www.linkedin.com/in/'],
      ];
      socialLinks.forEach(([label, handle, base]) => {
        if (!handle) return;
        const link = el('a', label + ' Â· @' + handle); link.href = base + encodeURIComponent(handle);
        link.target = '_blank'; link.rel = 'noopener noreferrer';
        contactDetails.append(link); hasContactDetails = true;
      });
      if (hasContactDetails) { contactDetails.prepend(el('h3', 'Work & contact')); profileInfo.append(contactDetails); }
      if (hasAboutDetails || hasContactDetails) {
        const dashboard = view.querySelector('#profileDashboard');
        if (dashboard) view.insertBefore(profileInfo, dashboard);
        else view.append(profileInfo);
      }

      if (pr.interests) {
        const tags = pr.interests.split(',').map(s => s.trim()).filter(Boolean).slice(0, 15);
        if (tags.length) {
          const box = el('section', undefined, 'profile-about-card profile-interests');
          box.append(el('h2', 'Interests'));
          const tagBox = el('div');
          tags.forEach(t => tagBox.append(el('span', t, 'chip')));
          box.append(tagBox);
          view.append(box);
        }
      }

      const gallery = el('section', undefined, 'profile-gallery');
      gallery.id = 'profilePosts';
      gallery.append(el('h2', 'Posts'));
      const postList = el('div', undefined, 'profile-post-list');
      if (!pr.posts.length) postList.append(el('p', 'No posts yet.', 'empty'));
      pr.posts.forEach(item => {
        const card = el('article', undefined, 'profile-post-card');
        if (item.media_type === 'video') {
          const video = el('video'); video.src = item.image_url; video.controls = true; video.preload = 'metadata'; video.playsInline = true; video.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Video posted by @' + pr.username); card.append(video);
        } else {
          const image = el('img'); image.src = item.image_url; image.loading = 'lazy'; image.alt = 'Photo posted by @' + pr.username; card.append(image);
        }
        const copy = el('div', undefined, 'profile-post-copy');
        copy.append(el('span', new Date(item.created_at).toLocaleString(), 'profile-post-date'));
        if (item.caption) copy.append(el('p', item.caption));
        card.append(copy); postList.append(card);
      });
      gallery.append(postList);
      view.append(gallery);
      const columns = el('div', undefined, 'profile-content-columns');
      const leftColumn = el('div', undefined, 'profile-left-column');
      const rightColumn = el('aside', undefined, 'profile-right-column');
      Array.from(view.children).forEach(child => {
        if (child === gallery) rightColumn.append(child);
        else if (!child.classList.contains('back') && !child.classList.contains('profile-hero') && !child.classList.contains('stats')) leftColumn.append(child);
      });
      columns.append(leftColumn, rightColumn);
      view.append(columns);
      if (pr.is_me && loggedIn && dashboardSection) {
        const scrollToDashboardTarget = () => requestAnimationFrame(() => {
          const targetId = {
            'dashboard-followers': 'profileFollowers',
            'dashboard-following': 'profileFollowing',
            'dashboard-admirers': 'profileAdmirers',
            'dashboard-requests': 'profileRequests',
            'dashboard-posts': 'profilePosts',
          }[dashboardSection] || dashboardSection;
          const target = document.getElementById(targetId);
          target?.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
          if (dashboardSection === 'dashboard-followers') followersButton.click();
          if (dashboardSection === 'dashboard-following') followingButton.click();
          if (dashboardSection === 'dashboard-admirers') admirationButton?.click();
        });
        if (dashboardSection.startsWith('dashboard-') && !document.getElementById(dashboardSection)) {
          document.getElementById('profileDashboard')?.addEventListener(
            'dashboard-ready',
            scrollToDashboardTarget,
            { once: true },
          );
        } else {
          scrollToDashboardTarget();
        }
      }
    }

    // ---------- composer / auth ----------
    function renderComposer() {
      const box = document.getElementById('composer');
      box.textContent = '';
      if (!loggedIn) return;

      const file = el('input');
      file.type = 'file';
      file.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,video/mp4,video/webm';
      const mediaHint = el('span', 'Photos: JPEG, PNG, WebP or GIF. Videos: MP4 or WebM, up to 50 MB.', 'note');
      const cap = el('input'); cap.type = 'text'; cap.placeholder = 'Caption';
      const tags = el('input'); tags.type = 'text'; tags.placeholder = 'Tag people (usernames, comma-separated; up to 10)';
      const msg = el('span'); msg.style.color = '#c00'; msg.style.fontSize = '12px';
      const btn = el('button', 'Post');

      btn.onclick = async () => {
        if (!file.files[0]) { msg.textContent = 'Choose a photo or video first'; return; }
        const fd = new FormData();
        fd.append('caption', cap.value);
        fd.append('tags', tags.value);
        fd.append('image', file.files[0]);
        btn.disabled = true;
        msg.textContent = 'Uploading...';
        const r = await fetch('/api/posts', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
        btn.disabled = false;
        if (r.ok) { file.value = ''; cap.value = ''; tags.value = ''; msg.textContent = ''; loadFeed(); loadActivity(); }
        else if (r.status === 413) msg.textContent = 'Video too large (max 50 MB).';
        else if (r.status === 415) msg.textContent = 'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF, MP4 or WebM file.';
        else if (r.status === 400) msg.textContent = 'Check the photo/video, caption length, and tagged usernames (up to 10 existing users)';
        else if (r.status === 401) msg.textContent = 'Please log in again';
        else msg.textContent = 'Something went wrong';
      };
      box.append(file, mediaHint, cap, tags, btn, msg);
    }

    async function renderAuth() {
      const box = document.getElementById('auth');
      box.textContent = '';
      try {
        const statusResponse = await fetch('/api/status');
        if (statusResponse.ok) appEnabled = await statusResponse.json();
      } catch { appEnabled = true; }
      const res = await fetch('/api/me');
      loggedIn = res.ok;
      renderComposer();

      if (res.ok) {
        const me = await res.json();
        currentUsername = me.username;
        currentIsAdmin = me.is_admin;
        currentIsSuperadmin = me.is_superadmin;
        setHomeCover(
          me.cover_image_url,
          me.cover_position_x,
          me.cover_position_y,
          me.cover_zoom,
        );
        const inbox = el('a', 'Chats', 'inbox-link');
        inbox.href = '#/inbox';
        const friends = el('a', 'Friends', 'inbox-link');
        friends.href = '#/friends';
        const communities = el('a', 'Community', 'inbox-link');
        communities.href = '#/community';
        const settings = el('a', 'Settings', 'inbox-link');
        settings.href = '#/settings';
        if (me.is_admin && (appEnabled || me.is_superadmin)) {
          const admin = el('a', 'Admin hub', 'inbox-link admin-link');
          admin.href = '#/admin';
          box.append(admin);
        }
        const out = el('button', 'Log out');
        out.onclick = async () => {
          await stopLiveLocationSharing(true);
          await post('/api/logout');
          renderAuth();
        };
        const mine = userLink('@' + me.username);
        mine.href = '#/u/' + encodeURIComponent(me.username);
        box.append(inbox, friends, communities, settings, mine, out);
      } else {
        currentUsername = '';
        currentIsAdmin = false;
        currentIsSuperadmin = false;
        setHomeCover(null);
        const u = el('input'); u.placeholder = 'username';
        const p = el('input'); p.placeholder = 'password'; p.type = 'password';
        const msg = el('span'); msg.id = 'msg';
        const login = el('button', 'Log in');
        const signup = el('a', 'Sign up', 'signup-link');
        signup.href = '#/signup';

        async function submit(path) {
          const r = await post(path, { username: u.value, password: p.value });
          if (r.ok) return renderAuth();
          if (r.status === 401) msg.textContent = 'Wrong username or password';
          else if (r.status === 400) msg.textContent = 'Enter a valid username and password';
          else msg.textContent = 'Something went wrong';
        }
        login.onclick = () => submit('/api/login');
        box.append(u, p, login, signup, msg);
      }
      if (!appEnabled && !currentIsSuperadmin) {
        ['homeView', 'profileView', 'inboxView', 'friendsView', 'communityView', 'userSearchView', 'settingsView', 'signupView', 'adminView']
          .forEach(id => { document.getElementById(id).hidden = true; });
        document.getElementById('appPausedView').hidden = false;
        return;
      }
      document.getElementById('appPausedView').hidden = true;
      const mapForm = document.getElementById('checkinForm'); if (mapForm) { mapForm.hidden = !loggedIn || !selectedMapPoint;
      document.getElementById('checkinLoginHint').hidden = loggedIn;
      document.getElementById('shareLocationButton').hidden = !loggedIn; }
      startPresenceTracking();
      route(); // re-draw the current page so it matches who is logged in
    }

    function stopInboxPolling() {
      if (inboxPoll !== null) {
        clearInterval(inboxPoll);
        inboxPoll = null;
      }
    }

    async function showInbox() {
      document.getElementById('homeView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('profileView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('settingsView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('userSearchView').hidden = true;
      document.getElementById('signupView').hidden = true;
      const view = document.getElementById('inboxView');
      view.hidden = false;
      view.textContent = '';
      stopInboxPolling();

      view.append(el('h1', 'Chats', 'inbox-title'));
      if (!loggedIn) {
        view.append(el('p', 'Log in to start a private conversation.', 'empty'));
        return;
      }

      const requestsPanel = el('section', undefined, 'chat-requests-panel');
      const requestsList = el('div', undefined, 'connections-list');
      requestsPanel.append(el('h2', 'Personal chat requests'), requestsList);
      view.append(requestsPanel);
      async function loadPersonalChatRequests() {
        requestsList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading requestsâ€¦', 'connection-empty'));
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/personal-chat-requests');
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
          const requests = await response.json();
          requestsList.replaceChildren();
          if (!requests.length) { requestsList.append(el('p', 'No personal chat requests yet.', 'connection-empty')); return; }
          requests.forEach(request => {
            const row = el('div', undefined, 'talk-request-row');
            const description = el('span');
            description.append(profileLink(request.username, '@' + request.username), el('span', ' Â· ' + (request.incoming ? 'Incoming' : 'Sent') + ' Â· ' + request.status, 'talk-request-status'));
            row.append(description);
            const actions = el('div', undefined, 'talk-request-actions');
            if (request.incoming && request.status === 'pending') {
              const accept = el('button', 'Accept');
              accept.onclick = async () => { accept.disabled = true; try { const result = await post('/api/personal-chat-requests/' + request.id + '/accept'); if (!result.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + result.status); const data = await result.json(); activeConversationId = data.conversation_id; location.hash = '#/inbox'; await showInbox(); } catch (error) { console.error('Could not accept chat request:', error); alert('Could not accept this request. Please try again.'); accept.disabled = false; } };
              const decline = el('button', 'Decline');
              decline.onclick = async () => { decline.disabled = true; try { const result = await post('/api/personal-chat-requests/' + request.id + '/reject'); if (!result.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + result.status); await loadPersonalChatRequests(); } catch (error) { console.error('Could not decline chat request:', error); alert('Could not decline this request. Please try again.'); decline.disabled = false; } };
              actions.append(accept, decline);
            } else if (request.status === 'accepted' && request.conversation_id) {
              const open = el('button', 'Open chat'); open.onclick = async () => { activeConversationId = request.conversation_id; await showInbox(); }; actions.append(open);
            }
            row.append(actions); requestsList.append(row);
          });
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not load personal chat requests:', error);
          requestsList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Could not load requests. Please try again.', 'connection-error'));
        }
      }
      await loadPersonalChatRequests();

      const layout = el('div', undefined, 'inbox-layout');
      const sidebar = el('section', undefined, 'conversation-sidebar');
      const sidebarHead = el('div', undefined, 'conversation-sidebar-head');
      sidebarHead.append(el('h2', 'Your chats'));
      const newButton = el('button', 'New message');
      const createForm = el('form', undefined, 'new-conversation');
      createForm.hidden = true;
      const recipientInput = el('input');
      recipientInput.type = 'text';
      recipientInput.placeholder = 'Search username, then choose a result';
      recipientInput.maxLength = 30;
      recipientInput.autocomplete = 'off';
      const recipientSuggestions = el('div', undefined, 'recipient-suggestions');
      recipientSuggestions.hidden = true;
      recipientSuggestions.setAttribute('role', 'listbox');
      const selectedRecipients = el('div', undefined, 'selected-recipients');
      const selectedUsernames = new Set();
      let groupMode = false;
      const titleInput = el('input');
      titleInput.type = 'text';
      titleInput.placeholder = 'Group name';
      titleInput.maxLength = 60;
      titleInput.hidden = true;
      const groupModeButton = el('button', 'Create group');
      groupModeButton.type = 'button';
      groupModeButton.onclick = () => {
        groupMode = true;
        titleInput.hidden = false;
        createSubmit.textContent = 'Create group';
        groupModeButton.hidden = true;
        createError.textContent = 'Select at least two people for a group chat.';
        recipientInput.focus();
      };
      const createError = el('span', undefined, 'hint');
      const createSubmit = el('button', 'Start chat');
      createForm.append(
        groupModeButton,
        recipientInput,
        recipientSuggestions,
        el('span', 'Type at least 2 letters, then select a matching username.', 'hint'),
        selectedRecipients,
        titleInput,
        createSubmit,
        createError,
      );
      let searchRequest = 0;
      function addRecipient(username) {
        const maxRecipients = groupMode ? 9 : 1;
        if (selectedUsernames.size >= maxRecipients) {
          createError.textContent = groupMode ? 'You can add up to 9 people.' : 'Choose Create group to add more than one person.';
          return;
        }
        selectedUsernames.add(username);
        recipientInput.value = '';
        recipientSuggestions.hidden = true;
        recipientSuggestions.textContent = '';
        createError.textContent = '';
        renderSelectedRecipients();
        recipientInput.focus();
      }
      recipientInput.oninput = async () => {
        const request = ++searchRequest;
        const query = recipientInput.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 2) {
          recipientSuggestions.hidden = true;
          recipientSuggestions.textContent = '';
          return;
        }
        recipientSuggestions.hidden = false;
        recipientSuggestions.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Searching...'));
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/users/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query));
          if (request !== searchRequest) return;
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('User search request failed.');
          const users = await response.json();
          const matches = users.filter(user => !selectedUsernames.has(user.username));
          recipientSuggestions.textContent = '';
          if (matches.length === 0) {
            recipientSuggestions.append(el('p', 'No matching users found.'));
            return;
          }
          matches.forEach(user => {
            const option = el('button', '@' + user.username);
            option.type = 'button';
            option.dataset.username = user.username;
            option.setAttribute('role', 'option');
            option.onclick = () => addRecipient(user.username);
            recipientSuggestions.append(option);
          });
        } catch {
          if (request !== searchRequest) return;
          recipientSuggestions.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Could not search users. Please try again.'));
        }
      };
      recipientInput.onkeydown = event => {
        if (event.key !== 'Enter') return;
        const query = recipientInput.value.trim();
        if (!query) return;
        event.preventDefault();
        const options = Array.from(recipientSuggestions.querySelectorAll('button'));
        const match = options.find(option =>
          option.dataset.username.toLowerCase() === query.toLowerCase());
        const option = match || (options.length === 1 ? options[0] : null);
        if (option) {
          option.click();
        } else if (options.length > 1) {
          options[0].focus();
        }
      };
      function renderSelectedRecipients() {
        selectedRecipients.textContent = '';
        selectedUsernames.forEach(username => {
          const chip = el('span', undefined, 'recipient-chip');
          const remove = el('button', 'Ã—');
          remove.type = 'button';
          remove.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Remove ' + username);
          remove.onclick = () => {
            selectedUsernames.delete(username);
            renderSelectedRecipients();
          };
          chip.append(el('span', '@' + username), remove);
          selectedRecipients.append(chip);
        });
      }
      newButton.onclick = () => {
        createForm.hidden = !createForm.hidden;
        if (!createForm.hidden) {
          groupMode = false;
          titleInput.hidden = true;
          titleInput.value = '';
          groupModeButton.hidden = false;
          selectedUsernames.clear();
          renderSelectedRecipients();
          createError.textContent = '';
        }
        createSubmit.textContent = 'Start chat';
        if (!createForm.hidden) recipientInput.focus();
      };
      createForm.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        const usernames = Array.from(selectedUsernames);
        if (usernames.length === 0 || (groupMode && usernames.length < 2)) {
          createError.textContent = groupMode ? 'Select at least two people for a group chat.' : 'Search for and select one person.';
          return;
        }
        createSubmit.disabled = true;
        createError.textContent = '';
        try {
          const response = await post('/api/conversations', {
            usernames,
            title: titleInput.value,
          });
          if (response.ok) {
            const result = await response.json();
            activeConversationId = result.id;
            selectedUsernames.clear();
            renderSelectedRecipients();
            recipientInput.value = '';
            titleInput.value = '';
            groupMode = false;
            titleInput.hidden = true;
            groupModeButton.hidden = false;
            createForm.hidden = true;
            chat.hidden = false;
            chat.classList.add('chat-window');
            await loadMessages(activeConversationId);
            await loadConversations();
          } else if (response.status === 400) {
            createError.textContent = 'Select 1â€“9 existing users other than yourself.';
          } else if (response.status === 401) {
            createError.textContent = 'Please log in again.';
          } else {
            createError.textContent = 'Could not start that conversation.';
          }
        } catch {
          createError.textContent = 'Could not start that conversation. Check your connection and try again.';
        } finally {
          createSubmit.disabled = false;
        }
      };
      const conversationList = el('div', undefined, 'conversation-list');
      let inboxFilter = 'all';
      let cachedConversations = [];
      const messageFilters = el('div', undefined, 'message-filters');
      const filterButtons = {};
      [['all', 'All'], ['unread', 'Unread'], ['groups', 'Groups'], ['community', 'Community']].forEach(([key, label]) => {
        const button = el('button', label, 'message-filter' + (key === inboxFilter ? ' active' : ''));
        button.type = 'button';
        button.onclick = () => {
          inboxFilter = key;
          Object.entries(filterButtons).forEach(([name, item]) => item.classList.toggle('active', name === key));
          renderConversationItems();
        };
        filterButtons[key] = button;
        messageFilters.append(button);
      });
      sidebarHead.append(newButton);
      sidebar.append(sidebarHead, createForm, messageFilters, conversationList);

      const chat = el('section', undefined, 'chat-pane');
      chat.hidden = true;
      const heading = el('div', undefined, 'chat-heading');
      const headingTitle = el('span', 'Choose a conversation', 'chat-heading-title');
      const chatActions = el('div', undefined, 'chat-actions');
      const chatMenu = el('details');
      const chatMenuSummary = el('summary', 'â‹¯');
      const chatMenuItems = el('div', undefined, 'chat-menu');
      const makeGroupAction = el('button', 'Create group chat');
      makeGroupAction.type = 'button';
      makeGroupAction.onclick = () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        if (!active) return;
        selectedUsernames.clear();
        active.members.filter(name => name !== currentUsername).forEach(name => selectedUsernames.add(name));
        renderSelectedRecipients();
        titleInput.value = '';
        groupMode = true;
        titleInput.hidden = false;
        groupModeButton.hidden = true;
        createForm.hidden = false;
        createSubmit.textContent = 'Create group';
        chatMenu.open = false;
        recipientInput.focus();
      };
      const viewProfileAction = el('button', 'View profile');
      viewProfileAction.type = 'button';
      viewProfileAction.onclick = () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        const username = active?.members.find(name => name !== currentUsername);
        if (username) location.hash = '#/u/' + encodeURIComponent(username);
      };
      const deleteChatAction = el('button', 'Delete chat from my inbox');
      deleteChatAction.type = 'button';
      deleteChatAction.onclick = async () => {
        if (!activeConversationId || !confirm('Remove this chat from your inbox?')) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + activeConversationId, { method: 'DELETE' });
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not remove this chat.'); return; }
        chatMenu.open = false;
        activeConversationId = null;
        chat.hidden = true;
        chat.classList.remove('chat-window');
        await loadConversations();
      };
      const renameGroupAction = el('button', 'Rename group');
      renameGroupAction.type = 'button';
      renameGroupAction.onclick = async () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        if (!active?.is_owner) return;
        const title = prompt('Enter a name for this group:', active.title || '');
        if (title === null || !title.trim()) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + active.id, {
          method: 'PUT', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ title: title.trim() }),
        });
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not rename this group.'); return; }
        chatMenu.open = false;
        await loadConversations();
      };
      const viewMembersAction = el('button', 'View group members');
      viewMembersAction.type = 'button';
      viewMembersAction.onclick = async () => {
        if (!activeConversationId) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + activeConversationId + '/members');
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not load group members.'); return; }
        const members = await response.json();
        alert('Group members:\n' + members.map(member => '@' + member.username + (member.is_owner ? ' (owner)' : '')).join('\n'));
        chatMenu.open = false;
      };
      const removeGroupMemberAction = el('button', 'Remove a member');
      removeGroupMemberAction.type = 'button';
      removeGroupMemberAction.onclick = async () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        if (!active?.is_owner) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + active.id + '/members');
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not load group members.'); return; }
        const members = (await response.json()).filter(member => !member.is_owner);
        if (!members.length) { alert('There are no other group members to remove.'); return; }
        const usernames = members.map(member => member.username);
        const username = prompt('Enter the username to remove:\n' + usernames.map(name => '@' + name).join('\n'));
        if (username === null) return;
        const member = members.find(item => item.username.toLowerCase() === username.trim().replace(/^@/, '').toLowerCase());
        if (!member) { alert('That username is not a removable member of this group.'); return; }
        if (!confirm('Remove @' + member.username + ' from this group?')) return;
        const removed = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + active.id + '/members/' + member.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
        if (!removed.ok) { alert('Could not remove that member.'); return; }
        chatMenu.open = false;
        await loadConversations();
      };
      const clearGroupMessagesAction = el('button', 'Delete all group messages');
      clearGroupMessagesAction.type = 'button';
      clearGroupMessagesAction.onclick = async () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        if (!active?.is_owner || !confirm('Permanently delete every message in this group?')) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + active.id + '/messages', { method: 'DELETE' });
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not delete the group messages.'); return; }
        chatMenu.open = false;
        await loadConversations();
        await loadMessages(active.id);
      };
      const deleteGroupAction = el('button', 'Delete group chat');
      deleteGroupAction.type = 'button';
      deleteGroupAction.onclick = async () => {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        if (!active?.is_owner || !confirm('Delete this group and its messages for every member? This cannot be undone.')) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + active.id + '/group', { method: 'DELETE' });
        if (!response.ok) { alert('Could not delete the group.'); return; }
        chatMenu.open = false;
        activeConversationId = null;
        chat.hidden = true;
        chat.classList.remove('chat-window');
        await loadConversations();
      };
      const reportChatAction = el('button', 'Report chat');
      reportChatAction.type = 'button';
      reportChatAction.onclick = async () => {
        if (!activeConversationId) return;
        const details = prompt('Why are you reporting this chat? (optional)') || '';
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + activeConversationId + '/report', {
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }, body: JSON.stringify({ details }),
        });
        chatMenu.open = false;
        alert(response.ok ? 'Your report was sent to the admin team.' : 'Could not report this chat.');
      };
      chatMenuItems.append(makeGroupAction, renameGroupAction, viewMembersAction, removeGroupMemberAction,
        clearGroupMessagesAction, deleteGroupAction, viewProfileAction, deleteChatAction, reportChatAction);
      function updateChatMenuActions() {
        const active = cachedConversations.find(item => item.id === activeConversationId);
        const isGroup = Boolean(active?.is_group);
        makeGroupAction.hidden = !active;
        renameGroupAction.hidden = !isGroup || !active.is_owner;
        viewMembersAction.hidden = !isGroup;
        removeGroupMemberAction.hidden = !isGroup || !active.is_owner;
        clearGroupMessagesAction.hidden = !isGroup || !active.is_owner;
        deleteGroupAction.hidden = !isGroup || !active.is_owner;
        viewProfileAction.hidden = isGroup || !active;
        deleteChatAction.hidden = !active || (isGroup && Boolean(active.is_owner));
        reportChatAction.hidden = !active;
        deleteChatAction.textContent = isGroup ? 'Leave group chat' : 'Delete chat from my inbox';
      }
      chatMenu.append(chatMenuSummary, chatMenuItems);
      updateChatMenuActions();
      const closeChat = el('button', 'Ã—');
      closeChat.type = 'button'; closeChat.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Close chat');
      closeChat.onclick = () => { chat.hidden = true; chat.classList.remove('chat-window'); };
      chatActions.append(chatMenu, closeChat);
      heading.append(headingTitle, chatActions);
      const messageList = el('div', undefined, 'message-list');
      messageList.append(el('p', 'Choose a conversation or start a new one.', 'message-empty'));
      const messageForm = el('form', undefined, 'message-composer');
      const messageInput = el('input');
      messageInput.type = 'text';
      messageInput.placeholder = 'Write a message...';
      messageInput.maxLength = 2000;
      const imageInput = el('input');
      imageInput.type = 'file';
      imageInput.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,video/mp4,video/webm';
      const emojiButton = el('button', 'â˜º', 'media-tool emoji-trigger'); emojiButton.type = 'button'; emojiButton.title = 'Add emoji';
      const gifButton = el('button', 'GIF', 'media-tool chat-media-trigger'); gifButton.type = 'button'; gifButton.title = 'Choose a GIF';
      const stickerButton = el('button', 'â–§', 'media-tool chat-media-trigger'); stickerButton.type = 'button'; stickerButton.title = 'Choose a sticker'; stickerButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Choose a sticker');
      const plusButton = el('button', '+', 'media-tool'); plusButton.type = 'button'; plusButton.title = 'Add a photo or video';
      const emojiTray = el('div', undefined, 'emoji-tray'); emojiTray.hidden = true;
      const mediaPicker = el('section', undefined, 'chat-media-picker'); mediaPicker.hidden = true;
      const pickerHead = el('div', undefined, 'chat-media-picker-head');
      const mediaSearch = el('input'); mediaSearch.type = 'search'; mediaSearch.placeholder = 'Search GIFs'; mediaSearch.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Search GIFs or stickers');
      const mediaSearchButton = el('button', 'Search'); mediaSearchButton.type = 'button';
      pickerHead.append(mediaSearch, mediaSearchButton);
      const mediaStatus = el('p', 'Search online GIFs and stickers to send.', 'chat-media-status');
      const mediaResults = el('div', undefined, 'chat-media-results');
      mediaPicker.append(pickerHead, mediaStatus, mediaResults);
      ['ðŸ˜€', 'ðŸ˜‚', 'ðŸ¥°', 'ðŸ˜', 'ðŸ˜Š', 'ðŸ˜‰', 'ðŸ˜­', 'ðŸ˜Ž', 'ðŸ¤—', 'â¤ï¸', 'ðŸ‘', 'ðŸŽ‰', 'ðŸ™', 'ðŸ”¥', 'âœ¨', 'ðŸ’¬'].forEach(emoji => {
        const pick = el('button', emoji); pick.type = 'button';
        pick.onclick = () => { messageInput.value += emoji; messageInput.focus(); };
        emojiTray.append(pick);
      });
      emojiButton.onclick = () => { mediaPicker.hidden = true; chatMenu.open = false; emojiTray.hidden = !emojiTray.hidden; };
      let mediaKind = 'gifs';
      async function searchChatMedia() {
        const query = mediaSearch.value.trim();
        if (query.length < 1) { mediaStatus.textContent = 'Enter a search term.'; return; }
        mediaSearchButton.disabled = true;
        mediaStatus.textContent = 'Searchingâ€¦';
        mediaResults.replaceChildren();
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/chat-media/search?q=' + encodeURIComponent(query) + '&kind=' + mediaKind);
          if (response.status === 503) throw new Error('Online media search is temporarily unavailable.');
          if (response.status === 502) throw new Error('GIPHY could not be reached or rejected the API key. Check the key and server internet connection.');
          if (response.status === 404) throw new Error('Restart the Rust app so it loads the GIF and sticker search endpoint.');
          if (response.status === 401) throw new Error('Please log in again to search GIFs and stickers.');
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('GIF search failed (server status ' + response.status + ').');
          const results = await response.json();
          if (!results.length) { mediaStatus.textContent = 'No results found.'; return; }
          const commonsResults = results.some(result => result.media_url.includes('wikimedia.org'));
          mediaStatus.textContent = commonsResults ? 'Choose one to send Â· Wikimedia Commons' : 'Choose one to send Â· GIPHY';
          results.forEach(result => {
            const pick = el('button'); pick.type = 'button'; pick.title = result.title || 'Send media';
            const preview = el('img'); preview.src = result.preview_url; preview.alt = result.title || (mediaKind === 'stickers' ? 'Sticker' : 'GIF'); preview.loading = 'lazy';
            pick.append(preview);
            pick.onclick = () => sendRemoteMedia(result.media_url);
            const tile = el('div', undefined, 'chat-media-tile'); tile.append(pick);
            if (result.source_url && result.media_url.includes('wikimedia.org')) { const credit = el('a', 'Source', 'chat-media-credit'); credit.href = result.source_url; credit.target = '_blank'; credit.rel = 'noopener noreferrer'; tile.append(credit); }
            mediaResults.append(tile);
          });
        } catch (error) { mediaStatus.textContent = error.message; }
        finally { mediaSearchButton.disabled = false; }
      }
      async function openMediaPicker(kind) {
        if (!mediaPicker.hidden && mediaKind === kind) { mediaPicker.hidden = true; return; }
        const kindChanged = mediaKind !== kind;
        mediaKind = kind;
        mediaSearch.placeholder = kind === 'stickers' ? 'Search stickers' : 'Search GIFs';
        mediaPicker.hidden = false;
        emojiTray.hidden = true;
        chatMenu.open = false;
        mediaSearch.focus();
        if (kindChanged) mediaResults.replaceChildren();
        if (kindChanged || !mediaResults.childElementCount) {
          if (!mediaSearch.value.trim()) mediaSearch.value = 'trending';
          await searchChatMedia();
        }
      }
      async function sendRemoteMedia(url) {
        if (activeConversationId === null) { messageHint.textContent = 'Choose a conversation first.'; return; }
        const data = new FormData(); data.append('body', messageInput.value); data.append('remote_media_url', url);
        sendButton.disabled = true; messageHint.textContent = 'Sendingâ€¦';
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + activeConversationId + '/messages', { method: 'POST', body: data });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error(response.status === 403 ? 'This chat is muted by an administrator.' : 'Could not send this media.');
          messageInput.value = ''; mediaPicker.hidden = true; mediaResults.replaceChildren();
          messageHint.textContent = 'Photos, GIFs, and videos up to 50 MB. Messages refresh every 5 seconds.';
          await loadConversations(); await loadMessages(activeConversationId);
        } catch (error) { messageHint.textContent = error.message; }
        finally { sendButton.disabled = false; }
      }
      gifButton.onclick = () => openMediaPicker('gifs');
      stickerButton.onclick = () => openMediaPicker('stickers');
      mediaSearchButton.onclick = searchChatMedia;
      mediaSearch.onkeydown = event => { if (event.key === 'Enter') { event.preventDefault(); searchChatMedia(); } };
      plusButton.onclick = () => { imageInput.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,video/mp4,video/webm'; imageInput.click(); };
      const sendButton = el('button', 'Send');
      sendButton.type = 'submit';
      messageForm.append(emojiButton, gifButton, stickerButton, plusButton, messageInput, imageInput, sendButton);
      const messageHint = el('p', 'Photos, GIFs, and videos up to 50 MB. Messages refresh every 5 seconds.', 'inbox-note');
      messageForm.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (activeConversationId === null) {
          messageHint.textContent = 'Choose or start a conversation first.';
          return;
        }
        if (!messageInput.value.trim() && !imageInput.files[0]) {
          messageHint.textContent = 'Write a message or choose a picture.';
          return;
        }
        const data = new FormData();
        data.append('body', messageInput.value);
        if (imageInput.files[0]) data.append('image', imageInput.files[0]);
        sendButton.disabled = true;
        sendButton.textContent = 'Sending...';
        messageHint.textContent = 'Sending your message...';
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + activeConversationId + '/messages', {
            method: 'POST',
            body: data,
          });
          if (response.ok) {
            messageInput.value = '';
            imageInput.value = '';
            messageHint.textContent = 'Photos, GIFs, and videos up to 50 MB. Messages refresh every 5 seconds.';
            await loadConversations();
            await loadMessages(activeConversationId);
          } else if (response.status === 413) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'Media too large (max 50 MB).';
          } else if (response.status === 415) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'Choose a JPEG, PNG, WebP, GIF, MP4, or WebM file.';
          } else if (response.status === 400) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'Write a message up to 2,000 characters, or attach a picture.';
          } else if (response.status === 401) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'Please log in again.';
          } else if (response.status === 403) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'This chat has been muted by an administrator.';
          } else if (response.status === 404) {
            messageHint.textContent = 'This conversation is no longer available. Select it again or start a new chat.';
          } else {
            messageHint.textContent = 'Message could not be sent (server error ' + response.status + '). Please try again.';
          }
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not send message:', error);
          messageHint.textContent = 'Message could not be sent. Check your connection and try again.';
        } finally {
          sendButton.disabled = false;
          sendButton.textContent = 'Send';
        }
      };
      chat.append(heading, messageList, emojiTray, mediaPicker, messageForm, messageHint);
      layout.append(sidebar, chat);
      view.append(layout);

      await loadConversations();
      if (activeConversationId !== null && !chat.hidden) await loadMessages(activeConversationId);
      inboxPoll = setInterval(async () => {
        await loadConversations();
        if (activeConversationId !== null && !chat.hidden) await loadMessages(activeConversationId);
      }, 5000);

      async function loadConversations() {
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations');
        if (!response.ok) {
          conversationList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Could not load your conversations.', 'activity-empty'));
          return;
        }
        cachedConversations = await response.json();
        updateChatMenuActions();
        conversationList.textContent = '';
        if (cachedConversations.length === 0) {
          conversationList.append(el('p', 'Your conversations will appear here.', 'activity-empty'));
          activeConversationId = null;
          headingTitle.textContent = 'Choose a conversation';
          messageList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Start a chat to say hello.', 'message-empty'));
          messageInput.disabled = false; sendButton.disabled = false;
          return;
        }
        if (!cachedConversations.some(conversation => conversation.id === activeConversationId)) {
          activeConversationId = cachedConversations[0].id;
        }
        renderConversationItems();
        if (activeConversationId !== null) {
          const active = cachedConversations.find(conversation => conversation.id === activeConversationId);
          if (active) headingTitle.textContent = active.title ||
            active.members.filter(name => name !== currentUsername).join(', ') || 'Conversation';
          const muted = Boolean(active?.is_muted);
          messageInput.disabled = muted;
          sendButton.disabled = muted;
          emojiButton.disabled = muted; gifButton.disabled = muted; stickerButton.disabled = muted; plusButton.disabled = muted;
          messageHint.textContent = muted ? 'This chat has been muted by an administrator. You can still view its messages.' : 'Photos, GIFs, and videos up to 50 MB. Messages refresh every 5 seconds.';
        }
        updateChatMenuActions();
      }

      function renderConversationItems() {
        conversationList.textContent = '';
        const visibleConversations = cachedConversations.filter(conversation => {
          if (inboxFilter === 'unread') return conversation.unread_count > 0;
          if (inboxFilter === 'groups') return conversation.is_group;
          if (inboxFilter === 'community') return conversation.is_group && /community/i.test(conversation.title || '');
          return true;
        });
        if (visibleConversations.length === 0) {
          conversationList.append(el('p', inboxFilter === 'unread' ? 'No unread messages.' :
            inboxFilter === 'community' ? 'No community chats yet.' : 'No chats in this view.', 'activity-empty'));
          return;
        }
        visibleConversations.forEach(conversation => {
          const item = el('button', undefined, 'conversation-item' +
            (conversation.id === activeConversationId ? ' active' : ''));
          const avatar = el('div', conversation.is_group ? 'G' : (conversation.members.find(name => name !== currentUsername) || '?')[0].toUpperCase(), 'activity-icon');
          const copy = el('span', undefined, 'conversation-copy');
          const title = conversation.title ||
            conversation.members.filter(name => name !== currentUsername).join(', ');
          const name = el('span', title || 'Conversation', 'conversation-name');
          if (conversation.is_muted) name.append(el('span', ' Â· Muted', 'conversation-unread'));
          if (conversation.unread_count > 0) name.append(el('span', String(conversation.unread_count), 'conversation-unread'));
          copy.append(name, el('span', conversation.latest_message || 'No messages yet', 'conversation-preview'));
          item.append(avatar, copy);
          item.onclick = async () => {
            activeConversationId = conversation.id;
            chat.hidden = false;
            chat.classList.add('chat-window');
            await loadMessages(conversation.id);
            await loadConversations();
          };
          conversationList.append(item);
        });
      }

      async function loadMessages(conversationId) {
        if (conversationId === null || conversationId !== activeConversationId) return;
        const response = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + conversationId + '/messages');
        if (!response.ok) {
          messageList.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Could not load messages for this conversation.', 'message-empty'));
          return;
        }
        const messages = await response.json();
        if (conversationId !== activeConversationId) return;
        messageList.textContent = '';
        if (messages.length === 0) {
          messageList.append(el('p', 'No messages yet. Say hello!', 'message-empty'));
          return;
        }
        messages.forEach(message => {
          const bubble = el('div', undefined, 'message-bubble' +
            (message.sender === currentUsername ? ' mine' : ''));
          if (message.sender !== currentUsername) {
            bubble.append(el('span', message.sender, 'message-sender'));
          }
          if (message.body) bubble.append(el('div', message.body));
          if (message.image_url) {
            if (message.media_type === 'video') {
              const video = el('video');
              video.src = message.image_url;
              video.controls = true;
              video.playsInline = true;
              video.className = 'message-media';
              bubble.append(video);
            } else {
              const image = el('img');
              image.src = message.image_url;
              image.alt = 'Media shared by ' + message.sender;
              image.className = 'message-media';
              bubble.append(image);
            }
          }
          bubble.append(el('span', new Date(message.created_at).toLocaleString(), 'message-time'));
          if (message.can_delete) {
            const remove = el('button', 'Delete', 'message-delete');
            remove.type = 'button';
            remove.title = 'Delete this message';
            remove.onclick = async () => {
              if (!confirm('Delete this message?')) return;
              const result = await fetch('/api/conversations/' + conversationId + '/messages/' + message.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
              if (!result.ok) { alert('Could not delete this message.'); return; }
              await loadConversations();
              await loadMessages(conversationId);
            };
            bubble.append(remove);
          }
          messageList.append(bubble);
        });
        messageList.scrollTop = messageList.scrollHeight;
      }
    }

    // ---------- feed ----------
    async function loadFeed() {
      const url = scope === 'following' ? '/api/feed?scope=following' : '/api/feed';
      const posts = await (await fetch(url)).json();
      const feed = document.getElementById('feed');
      feed.textContent = '';

      if (posts.length === 0) {
        feed.append(el('p', scope === 'following'
          ? 'Nothing here yet. Click a username to visit a profile and follow them.'
          : 'No posts yet.', 'empty'));
        return;
      }

      posts.forEach(p => {
        const card = el('div', undefined, 'card');
        const user = el('div', undefined, 'user');
        const author = el('span', undefined, 'post-author');
        author.append(userLink(p.user));
        const icon = moodIcon(p.mood);
        if (icon) {
          const mood = el('span', icon, 'post-author-mood');
          mood.title = p.mood;
          mood.setAttribute('role', 'img');
          mood.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Mood: ' + p.mood);
          author.append(mood);
        }
        user.append(avatarEl(p.avatar_url), author);
        if (loggedIn) {
          const userControls = el('div', undefined, 'post-user-controls');
          const userActions = el('details', undefined, 'post-user-actions');
          const userActionsLabel = el('summary', 'Actions');
          userActionsLabel.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Post actions for @' + p.user);
          const userMenu = el('div', undefined, 'post-user-menu');
          const actionStatus = el('p', undefined, 'report-status');

          if (p.user !== currentUsername) {
            const followButton = el('button', p.followed_by_me ? 'Unfollow' : 'Follow', 'post-follow-button');
            followButton.type = 'button';
            followButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(p.followed_by_me));
            const followStatus = el('span', undefined, 'post-follow-status');
            followStatus.setAttribute('role', 'status');
            followButton.onclick = async () => {
              followButton.disabled = true;
              followStatus.textContent = '';
              try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(p.user) + '/follow', {
                  method: p.followed_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
                });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Follow request failed: ' + response.status);
                p.followed_by_me = !p.followed_by_me;
                window.followSync(p.user, p.followed_by_me);
                followButton.textContent = p.followed_by_me ? 'Unfollow' : 'Follow';
                followButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(p.followed_by_me));
                followStatus.textContent = p.followed_by_me ? 'Following @' + p.user : 'Unfollowed @' + p.user;
              } catch (error) {
                console.error('Could not update follow status:', error);
                followStatus.textContent = 'Follow could not be updated (' + error.message + ').';
              } finally {
                followButton.disabled = false;
              }
            };
            userControls.append(followButton, followStatus);
            window.followRegistry = window.followRegistry || {};
            window.followSync = window.followSync || ((user, state) => {
              const list = (window.followRegistry[user] || []).filter(x => x.btn.isConnected);
              window.followRegistry[user] = list;
              list.forEach(x => x.fn(state));
            });
            (window.followRegistry[p.user] = window.followRegistry[p.user] || []).push({
              btn: followButton,
              fn: state => {
                p.followed_by_me = state;
                followButton.textContent = state ? 'Unfollow' : 'Follow';
                followButton.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(state));
              },
            });

            const favoriteButton = el('button', p.favorited_by_me ? 'Remove from favorites' : 'Add to favorites');
            favoriteButton.type = 'button';
            favoriteButton.onclick = async () => {
              favoriteButton.disabled = true;
              actionStatus.textContent = '';
              try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(p.user) + '/favorite', {
                  method: p.favorited_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
                });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Favorite request failed: ' + response.status);
                p.favorited_by_me = !p.favorited_by_me;
                favoriteButton.textContent = p.favorited_by_me ? 'Remove from favorites' : 'Add to favorites';
                actionStatus.textContent = p.favorited_by_me
                  ? '@' + p.user + ' added to favorites'
                  : '@' + p.user + ' removed from favorites';
              } catch (error) {
                console.error('Could not update favorite status:', error);
                actionStatus.textContent = 'Favorite could not be updated. Please try again.';
              } finally {
                favoriteButton.disabled = false;
              }
            };
            userMenu.append(favoriteButton);
          }

          const reportButton = el('button', 'Report post');
          reportButton.type = 'button';
          const reportForm = el('form', undefined, 'report-form');
          reportForm.hidden = true;
          const reasonLabel = el('label', 'Why are you reporting this post?');
          const reasonSelect = el('select');
          [
            ['spam', 'Spam or misleading'],
            ['harassment', 'Harassment or bullying'],
            ['hate', 'Hateful content'],
            ['nudity', 'Nudity or sexual content'],
            ['violence', 'Violence or dangerous content'],
            ['scam', 'Scam or fraud'],
            ['other', 'Something else'],
          ].forEach(([value, label]) => {
            const option = el('option', label);
            option.value = value;
            reasonSelect.append(option);
          });
          reasonLabel.append(reasonSelect);
          const detailsLabel = el('label', 'Details (optional, up to 500 characters)');
          const reportDetails = el('textarea');
          reportDetails.maxLength = 500;
          detailsLabel.append(reportDetails);
          const reportSubmit = el('button', 'Submit report');
          reportSubmit.type = 'submit';
          const reportStatus = el('p', undefined, 'report-status');
          let reportWasSubmitted = false;
          reportForm.append(reasonLabel, detailsLabel, reportSubmit, reportStatus);
          reportButton.onclick = () => {
            reportForm.hidden = !reportForm.hidden;
            reportStatus.textContent = '';
          };
          reportForm.onsubmit = async event => {
            event.preventDefault();
            reportSubmit.disabled = true;
            reportStatus.textContent = 'Submitting report...';
            try {
              const response = await post('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/report', {
                reason: reasonSelect.value,
                details: reportDetails.value,
              });
              if (response.status === 409) {
                reportStatus.textContent = 'You have already reported this post.';
              } else if (response.status === 401) {
                reportStatus.textContent = 'Please log in again.';
              } else if (response.status === 400) {
                reportStatus.textContent = 'Please choose a valid reason and keep details under 500 characters.';
              } else if (!response.ok) {
                throw new Error('Post report failed: ' + response.status);
              } else {
                reportStatus.textContent = 'Report submitted. Thank you.';
                reportWasSubmitted = true;
              }
            } catch (error) {
              console.error('Could not submit post report:', error);
              reportStatus.textContent = 'Could not submit report. Please try again.';
            } finally {
              reportSubmit.disabled = reportWasSubmitted;
            }
          };
          userMenu.append(reportButton, reportForm, actionStatus);
          userActions.append(userActionsLabel, userMenu);
          userControls.append(userActions);
          user.append(userControls);
        }

        let media;
        if (p.media_type === 'video') {
          media = el('video', undefined, 'photo');
          media.src = p.image_url;
          media.controls = true;
          media.preload = 'metadata';
          media.playsInline = true;
        } else {
          media = el('img', undefined, 'photo');
          media.src = p.image_url;
          media.loading = 'lazy';
          media.alt = 'Photo posted by @' + p.user;
        }

        const mediaStage = el('div', undefined, 'media-stage');
        const reactions = el('div', undefined, 'media-reactions');
        const heart = el('button', undefined, 'like');
        heart.type = 'button';
        heart.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Like post');
        const dislike = el('button', undefined, 'dislike');
        dislike.type = 'button';
        const thumb = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'svg');
        thumb.setAttribute('viewBox', '0 0 24 24');
        thumb.setAttribute('transform', 'rotate(180 12 12)');
        thumb.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');
        const thumbPath = document.createElementNS('http://www.w3.org/2000/svg', 'path');
        thumbPath.setAttribute('d', 'M1 21h4V9H1v12Zm22-11a2 2 0 0 0-2-2h-6.31l.95-4.57.03-.32c0-.41-.17-.79-.44-1.06L14.17 1 7.59 7.59C7.22 7.95 7 8.45 7 9v10a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h9c.83 0 1.54-.5 1.84-1.22l3.02-7.05c.09-.23.14-.48.14-.73v-2Z');
        thumb.append(thumbPath);
        dislike.append(thumb);
        dislike.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Dislike post');
        const hate = el('button', 'Hate', 'hate');
        hate.type = 'button';
        hate.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Hate post');
        heart.title = 'Like';
        dislike.title = 'Dislike';
        hate.title = 'Hate';
        [heart, dislike, hate].forEach(button => {
