          button.addEventListener('click', event => event.stopPropagation());
        });
        const reshare = el('button', undefined, 'reshare');
        reshare.type = 'button';
        const voteCount = el('span', undefined, 'vote-count');
        const hint = el('span', undefined, 'hint');
        reactions.append(heart, dislike, hate);
        mediaStage.append(media, reactions);
        const actions = el('div', undefined, 'actions');
        actions.append(voteCount);
        if (loggedIn) actions.append(reshare);
        actions.append(hint);

        const meta = el('div', undefined, 'meta');
        meta.append(userLink(p.user), document.createTextNode(' ' + p.caption));
        if (p.original_author) {
          const reshareLabel = el('div', undefined, 'post-tags');
          reshareLabel.append(
            document.createTextNode('Reshared from '),
            profileLink(p.original_author, '@' + p.original_author),
          );
          meta.append(reshareLabel);
        }
        if (p.tags && p.tags.length) {
          const tagLine = el('div', undefined, 'post-tags');
          tagLine.append(el('span', 'With '));
          p.tags.forEach((name, index) => {
            if (index) tagLine.append(document.createTextNode(', '));
            tagLine.append(profileLink(name, '@' + name));
          });
          meta.append(tagLine);
        }

        function paint() {
          heart.textContent = p.liked_by_me ? 'â™¥' : 'â™¡';
          heart.style.color = p.liked_by_me ? '#e0245e' : '';
          heart.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(p.liked_by_me));
          dislike.classList.toggle('selected', p.disliked_by_me);
          dislike.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(p.disliked_by_me));
          dislike.setAttribute('aria-label', p.disliked_by_me ? 'Remove dislike' : 'Dislike post');
          hate.classList.toggle('selected', p.hated_by_me);
          hate.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(p.hated_by_me));
          hate.setAttribute('aria-label', p.hated_by_me ? 'Remove hate' : 'Hate post');
          voteCount.textContent = likesText(p.likes) + ' Â· ' + p.dislikes +
            (p.dislikes === 1 ? ' dislike' : ' dislikes') + ' Â· ' + p.hates +
            (p.hates === 1 ? ' hate' : ' hates') + ' Â· ' + p.reshares +
            (p.reshares === 1 ? ' reshare' : ' reshares');
          if (loggedIn) {
            reshare.textContent = p.reshared_by_me ? 'Undo reshare' : 'â†— Reshare';
            reshare.disabled = false;
            reshare.setAttribute('aria-label', p.reshared_by_me ? 'Undo reshare' : 'Reshare post');
          }
        }
        paint();

        heart.onclick = async () => {
          if (!loggedIn) { hint.textContent = 'Log in to like posts'; return; }
          hint.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/like', {
              method: p.liked_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
            });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Like request failed: ' + response.status);
            if (p.liked_by_me) {
              p.likes -= 1;
              p.liked_by_me = false;
            } else {
              if (p.disliked_by_me) p.dislikes -= 1;
              p.disliked_by_me = false;
              p.likes += 1;
              p.liked_by_me = true;
            }
            paint();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not update like:', error);
            hint.textContent = 'Like could not be updated. Please try again.';
          }
        };

        dislike.onclick = async () => {
          if (!loggedIn) { hint.textContent = 'Log in to react to posts'; return; }
          hint.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/dislike', {
              method: p.disliked_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
            });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Dislike request failed: ' + response.status);
            if (p.disliked_by_me) {
              p.dislikes -= 1;
              p.disliked_by_me = false;
            } else {
              if (p.liked_by_me) p.likes -= 1;
              p.liked_by_me = false;
              p.dislikes += 1;
              p.disliked_by_me = true;
            }
            paint();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not update dislike:', error);
            hint.textContent = 'Dislike could not be updated. Please try again.';
          }
        };

        hate.onclick = async () => {
          if (!loggedIn) { hint.textContent = 'Log in to react to posts'; return; }
          hint.textContent = '';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/hate', {
              method: p.hated_by_me ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
            });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Hate request failed: ' + response.status);
            p.hates += p.hated_by_me ? -1 : 1;
            p.hated_by_me = !p.hated_by_me;
            paint();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not update hate reaction:', error);
            hint.textContent = 'Hate reaction could not be updated. Please try again.';
          }
        };

        reshare.onclick = async () => {
          if (!loggedIn) { hint.textContent = 'Log in to reshare posts'; return; }
          reshare.disabled = true;
          const undo = p.reshared_by_me;
          hint.textContent = undo ? 'Undoing reshare...' : 'Resharing...';
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/reshare', {
              method: undo ? 'DELETE' : 'POST',
            });
            if (response.status === 409) {
              p.reshared_by_me = true;
              hint.textContent = 'You have already reshared this post.';
              paint();
              return;
            }
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('Reshare request failed: ' + response.status);
            p.reshared_by_me = !undo;
            p.reshares += undo ? -1 : 1;
            hint.textContent = undo ? 'Reshare removed.' : 'Reshared to your feed.';
            paint();
            await loadFeed();
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not reshare post:', error);
            hint.textContent = undo
              ? 'Reshare could not be removed. Please try again.'
              : 'Post could not be reshared. Please try again.';
            reshare.disabled = false;
          }
        };

        // ---- comments ----
        const cbtn = el('button', undefined, 'linkbtn');
        const cbox = el('div', undefined, 'comments');
        cbox.hidden = true;
        let loaded = false;

        function paintComments() {
          cbtn.textContent = p.comment_count === 0
            ? 'Add a comment'
            : 'View comments (' + p.comment_count + ')';
        }
        paintComments();

        cbtn.onclick = async () => {
          if (!cbox.hidden) { cbox.hidden = true; return; }
          cbox.hidden = false;
          if (loaded) return;
          loaded = true;

          const list = el('div');
          cbox.append(list);
          const res = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/comments');
          if (res.ok) (await res.json()).forEach(c => list.append(commentRow(c, p, () => { p.comment_count = Math.max(0, p.comment_count - 1); paintComments(); })));

          if (!loggedIn) {
            cbox.append(el('span', 'Log in to comment', 'hint'));
            return;
          }
          const form = el('div', undefined, 'newcomment');
          const input = el('input'); input.type = 'text'; input.placeholder = 'Add a comment... Use @username to mention someone';
          const send = el('button', 'Post');
          const err = el('span', undefined, 'hint');
          send.onclick = async () => {
            const r = await post('/api/posts/' + p.id + '/comments', { body: input.value });
            if (r.ok) {
              list.append(commentRow(await r.json(), p, () => { p.comment_count = Math.max(0, p.comment_count - 1); paintComments(); }));
              input.value = '';
              err.textContent = '';
              p.comment_count += 1;
              paintComments();
              loadActivity();
            } else if (r.status === 400) err.textContent = 'Comments must be 1-300 characters';
            else if (r.status === 401) err.textContent = 'Please log in again';
            else err.textContent = 'Something went wrong';
          };
          form.append(input, send);
          cbox.append(form, err);
        };

        if (p.user === currentUsername) {
            const deleteButton = el('button', 'Delete', 'post-delete-button');
            deleteButton.type = 'button';
            deleteButton.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Delete this post');
            deleteButton.onclick = async () => {
              if (!confirm('Delete this post? This cannot be undone.')) return;
              deleteButton.disabled = true;
              try {
                const response = await fetch('/api/posts/' + p.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
                if (!response.ok) throw new Error('status ' + response.status);
                card.remove();
              } catch (error) {
                console.error('Could not delete post:', error);
                deleteButton.disabled = false;
                deleteButton.textContent = 'Delete failed - retry';
              }
            };
            actions.append(deleteButton);
          }
          card.append(user, mediaStage, actions, meta, cbtn, cbox);
        feed.append(card);
      });
    }

    function checkinCard(checkin) {
      const card = el('article', undefined, 'checkin-card');
      const user = el('div', undefined, 'checkin-user');
      user.append(avatarEl(checkin.avatar_url), profileLink(checkin.username, '@' + checkin.username));
      if (checkin.username === currentUsername) {
        user.append(el('span', 'You', 'checkin-relation'));
      } else {
        if (checkin.is_following) user.append(el('span', 'Following', 'checkin-relation'));
        if (checkin.is_follower) user.append(el('span', 'Follower', 'checkin-relation'));
      }
      card.append(
        user,
        el('p', checkin.place, 'checkin-place'),
        el('p', checkinCategories.find(category => category.key === checkin.category)?.label || checkin.category, 'checkin-category'),
      );
      if (checkin.note) card.append(el('p', checkin.note, 'checkin-detail'));
      const meta = el('div', undefined, 'checkin-meta');
      meta.append(el('span', timeAgo(checkin.created_at), 'activity-time'));
      if (checkin.username === currentUsername) {
        const remove = el('button', 'Remove', 'checkin-remove');
        remove.type = 'button';
        remove.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Remove check-in at ' + checkin.place);
        remove.onclick = async () => {
          remove.disabled = true;
          try {
            const response = await fetch('/api/checkins/' + checkin.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
            if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
            await loadCheckins(true);
          } catch (error) {
            console.error('Could not remove check-in:', error);
            remove.disabled = false;
            document.getElementById('checkinFeedback').textContent =
              'Could not remove your check-in. Please try again.';
          }
        };
        meta.append(remove);
      }
      card.append(meta);
      return card;
    }

    function mapPopupLink(username) {
      const content = el('div', undefined, 'map-popup');
      content.append(profileLink(username, '@' + username));
      return content;
    }

    function checkinMarkerIcon(categoryKey) {
      const category = checkinCategories.find(item => item.key === categoryKey);
      return L.divIcon({
        className: '',
        html: '<span class="map-pin"><span>' + (category?.icon || 'âœ¦') + '</span></span>',
        iconSize: [30, 30],
        iconAnchor: [15, 30],
      });
    }

    function renderCheckinMarker(checkin) {
      if (
        !communityMap
        || !checkinMarkerLayer
        || !Number.isFinite(checkin.latitude)
        || !Number.isFinite(checkin.longitude)
      ) return;
      const marker = L.marker([checkin.latitude, checkin.longitude], {
        icon: checkinMarkerIcon(checkin.category),
      });
      const popup = mapPopupLink(checkin.username);
      popup.append(
        el('strong', checkin.place),
        el('span', checkinCategories.find(item => item.key === checkin.category)?.label || checkin.category),
        el('span', timeAgo(checkin.created_at)),
      );
      marker.bindPopup(popup).addTo(checkinMarkerLayer);
    }

    function initCommunityMap() {
      if (!document.getElementById('checkinMap')) return;
      const mapElement = document.getElementById('checkinMap');
      const unavailable = document.getElementById('mapUnavailable');
      const categoryList = document.getElementById('mapCategoryFilters');
      const select = document.getElementById('checkinCategory');
      const form = document.getElementById('checkinForm');
      const shareButton = document.getElementById('shareLocationButton');
      const feedback = document.getElementById('checkinFeedback');
      const sharingStatus = document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus');

      checkinCategories.forEach(category => {
        if (category.key !== 'all') {
          const option = el('option', category.label);
          option.value = category.key;
          select.append(option);
        }
        const filter = el('button', category.label, 'map-category-filter');
        filter.type = 'button';
        filter.dataset.category = category.key;
        filter.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(category.key === selectedCheckinCategory));
        filter.onclick = () => {
          selectedCheckinCategory = category.key;
          categoryList.querySelectorAll('button').forEach(button => {
            button.setAttribute('aria-pressed', String(button.dataset.category === category.key));
          });
          if (category.key !== 'all') select.value = category.key;
          loadCheckins(true);
        };
        categoryList.append(filter);
      });

      if (!window.L) {
        unavailable.hidden = false;
        mapElement.hidden = true;
      } else {
        communityMap = L.map(mapElement).setView([20, 0], 2);
        const tiles = L.tileLayer('https://{s}.tile.openstreetmap.org/{z}/{x}/{y}.png', {
          maxZoom: 19,
          attribution: '&copy; <a href="https://www.openstreetmap.org/copyright">OpenStreetMap contributors</a>',
        }).addTo(communityMap);
        tiles.on('tileerror', () => { unavailable.hidden = false; });
        checkinMarkerLayer = L.layerGroup().addTo(communityMap);
        liveLocationMarkerLayer = L.layerGroup().addTo(communityMap);
        communityMap.on('click', event => {
          selectedMapPoint = {
            latitude: Number(event.latlng.lat.toFixed(6)),
            longitude: Number(event.latlng.lng.toFixed(6)),
          };
          if (selectedCheckinMarker) selectedCheckinMarker.remove();
          selectedCheckinMarker = L.marker(
            [selectedMapPoint.latitude, selectedMapPoint.longitude],
          ).addTo(communityMap);
          document.getElementById('checkinCoordinates').textContent =
            'Selected: ' + selectedMapPoint.latitude + ', ' + selectedMapPoint.longitude;
          document.getElementById('checkinSubmit').disabled = !loggedIn;
          form.hidden = !loggedIn;
          document.getElementById('checkinLoginHint').hidden = loggedIn;
          if (loggedIn) document.getElementById('checkinPlace').focus();
        });
        setTimeout(() => communityMap.invalidateSize(), 0);
      }

      document.getElementById('checkinMore').onclick = () => loadCheckins(false);
      form.onsubmit = async event => {
        event.preventDefault();
        if (!selectedMapPoint) {
          feedback.textContent = 'Choose a point on the map first.';
          return;
        }
        const submit = document.getElementById('checkinSubmit');
        submit.disabled = true;
        feedback.textContent = 'Sharing your check-in...';
        try {
          const response = await post('/api/checkins', {
            category: select.value,
            place: document.getElementById('checkinPlace').value,
            note: document.getElementById('checkinNote').value,
            ...selectedMapPoint,
          });
          if (!response.ok) {
            feedback.textContent = response.status === 400
              ? 'Add a place name and check that the details are within the limits.'
              : response.status === 401
                ? 'Please log in again.'
                : 'Could not share your check-in.';
            submit.disabled = false;
            return;
          }
          document.getElementById('checkinPlace').value = '';
          document.getElementById('checkinNote').value = '';
          feedback.textContent = 'Your map check-in is visible to the community.';
          selectedMapPoint = null;
          if (selectedCheckinMarker) selectedCheckinMarker.remove();
          selectedCheckinMarker = null;
          form.hidden = true;
          await loadCheckins(true);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not create map check-in:', error);
          feedback.textContent = 'Could not share your check-in. Check your connection and try again.';
          submit.disabled = false;
        }
      };

      shareButton.onclick = () => {
        if (liveLocationWatchId === null) startLiveLocationSharing();
        else stopLiveLocationSharing(true);
      };
      document.getElementById('checkinLoginHint').hidden = loggedIn;
      shareButton.hidden = !loggedIn;
      loadCheckins(true);
      refreshLiveLocations();
      if (liveMapRefreshInterval === null) {
        liveMapRefreshInterval = setInterval(refreshLiveLocations, 15000);
      }
      window.addEventListener('pagehide', stopLiveLocationBeacon);
    }

    function updateCurrentLocationMarker(position) {
      if (!communityMap || !liveLocationMarkerLayer) return;
      const icon = L.divIcon({
        className: '',
        html: '<span class="map-live-pin" aria-hidden="true">You</span>',
        iconSize: [38, 38],
        iconAnchor: [19, 19],
      });
      liveLocationMarkerLayer.eachLayer(marker => {
        if (marker.options?.title === currentUsername) marker.remove();
      });
      L.marker([position.latitude, position.longitude], {
        icon,
        title: currentUsername,
      }).bindPopup('Your live location').addTo(liveLocationMarkerLayer);
    }

    async function publishLiveLocation(position) {
      const coords = position.coords || position;
      latestLivePosition = {
        latitude: coords.latitude,
        longitude: coords.longitude,
      };
      updateCurrentLocationMarker(latestLivePosition);
      if (Date.now() - lastLivePositionSentAt < 12000) return;
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/me/location', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(latestLivePosition),
        });
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
        lastLivePositionSentAt = Date.now();
        document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus').textContent =
          'Sharing your live location with all signed-in members.';
        await refreshLiveLocations();
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not update live location:', error);
        document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus').textContent =
          'Location update failed; retrying while sharing remains on.';
      }
    }

    function startLiveLocationSharing() {
      const button = document.getElementById('shareLocationButton');
      const status = document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus');
      if (!navigator.geolocation) {
        status.textContent = 'This browser does not support location sharing.';
        return;
      }
      button.disabled = true;
      status.textContent = 'Requesting browser location permission...';
      try {
        liveLocationWatchId = navigator.geolocation.watchPosition(
          position => {
            publishLiveLocation(position);
            if (communityMap && latestLivePosition) {
              communityMap.setView(
                [latestLivePosition.latitude, latestLivePosition.longitude],
                Math.max(communityMap.getZoom(), 14),
              );
            }
          },
          error => {
            console.error('Live location permission or update failed:', error);
            const errorMessage = error.code === error.PERMISSION_DENIED
              ? 'Location permission was denied. You can enable it in browser settings.'
              : 'Could not get your location. Please try again.';
            stopLiveLocationSharing(true).finally(() => {
              status.textContent = errorMessage;
            });
          },
          { enableHighAccuracy: true, maximumAge: 5000, timeout: 20000 },
        );
        button.textContent = 'Stop sharing my location';
        button.disabled = false;
        if (liveLocationRefreshInterval === null) {
          liveLocationRefreshInterval = setInterval(() => {
            if (latestLivePosition) publishLiveLocation({
              coords: latestLivePosition,
            });
          }, 20000);
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not start live location sharing:', error);
        liveLocationWatchId = null;
        button.disabled = false;
        status.textContent = 'Could not start location sharing. Please try again.';
      }
    }

    async function stopLiveLocationSharing(removeFromServer) {
      if (liveLocationWatchId !== null && navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(liveLocationWatchId);
      }
      liveLocationWatchId = null;
      latestLivePosition = null;
      lastLivePositionSentAt = 0;
      if (liveLocationRefreshInterval !== null) {
        clearInterval(liveLocationRefreshInterval);
        liveLocationRefreshInterval = null;
      }
      const button = document.getElementById('shareLocationButton');
      const status = document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus');
      if (button) {
        button.textContent = 'Share my live location';
        button.disabled = false;
      }
      if (liveLocationMarkerLayer) liveLocationMarkerLayer.clearLayers();
      let removalFailed = false;
      if (removeFromServer && loggedIn) {
        try {
          const response = await fetch('/api/me/location', { method: 'DELETE' });
          if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
        } catch (error) {
          console.error('Could not stop live location sharing:', error);
          removalFailed = true;
        }
      }
      if (status) {
        status.textContent = removalFailed
          ? 'Could not stop sharing on the server; your location will expire shortly.'
          : 'Live location sharing is off.';
      }
      await refreshLiveLocations(false);
    }

    function stopLiveLocationBeacon() {
      if (liveLocationWatchId !== null && navigator.geolocation) {
        navigator.geolocation.clearWatch(liveLocationWatchId);
        liveLocationWatchId = null;
      }
      if (liveLocationRefreshInterval !== null) {
        clearInterval(liveLocationRefreshInterval);
        liveLocationRefreshInterval = null;
      }
      if (latestLivePosition && navigator.sendBeacon) {
        navigator.sendBeacon('/api/me/location/stop', new Blob([], { type: 'text/plain' }));
      }
    }

    async function refreshLiveLocations(updateStatus = true) {
      if (!liveLocationMarkerLayer) return;
      if (!loggedIn) {
        liveLocationMarkerLayer.clearLayers();
        return;
      }
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/locations/live');
        if (response.status === 401) {
          liveLocationMarkerLayer.clearLayers();
          return;
        }
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
        const locations = await response.json();
        liveLocationMarkerLayer.clearLayers();
        locations.forEach(location => {
          const icon = L.divIcon({
            className: '',
            html: '<span class="map-live-pin" aria-hidden="true">' +
              (location.username === currentUsername ? 'You' : 'â—') + '</span>',
            iconSize: [38, 38],
            iconAnchor: [19, 19],
          });
          const marker = L.marker([location.latitude, location.longitude], { icon });
          const popup = location.username === currentUsername
            ? el('div', 'Your live location is shared with all signed-in members.', 'map-popup')
            : mapPopupLink(location.username);
          if (location.username !== currentUsername) popup.append(el('span', 'Sharing live location'));
          if (location.mood) popup.append(el('span', 'Mood: ' + location.mood));
          marker.bindPopup(popup).addTo(liveLocationMarkerLayer);
        });
        if (updateStatus) {
          document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus').textContent =
            locations.length
              ? locations.length + ' member' + (locations.length === 1 ? '' : 's') + ' sharing live location.'
              : 'Live locations only appear when a member chooses to share.';
        }
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not load live locations:', error);
        if (updateStatus) {
          document.getElementById('locationSharingStatus').textContent =
            'Live locations could not be refreshed. Check your connection.';
        }
      }
    }

    async function loadCheckins(reset) {
      const list = document.getElementById('checkinFeed');
      const more = document.getElementById('checkinMore');
      const sequence = reset ? ++checkinRequestSequence : checkinRequestSequence;
      const offset = reset ? 0 : checkinOffset;
      if (reset) {
        checkinOffset = 0;
        if (checkinMarkerLayer) checkinMarkerLayer.clearLayers();
        list.replaceChildren(el('p', 'Loading public check-ins...', 'checkin-empty'));
        more.hidden = true;
      } else {
        more.disabled = true;
        more.textContent = 'Loading...';
      }
      const params = new URLSearchParams();
      if (selectedCheckinCategory !== 'all') params.set('category', selectedCheckinCategory);
      params.set('offset', String(offset));
      try {
        const response = await fetch('/api/checkins?' + params.toString());
        if (!response.ok) throw new Error('HTTP ' + response.status);
        const checkins = await response.json();
        if (sequence !== checkinRequestSequence) return;
        if (reset) list.textContent = '';
        if (checkins.length === 0 && reset) {
          list.replaceChildren(
            el('p', 'No check-ins here yet. Be the first to share one.', 'checkin-empty'),
          );
        } else {
          checkins.forEach(checkin => {
            list.append(checkinCard(checkin));
            renderCheckinMarker(checkin);
          });
        }
        checkinOffset = offset + checkins.length;
        more.hidden = checkins.length < 50;
      } catch (error) {
        console.error('Could not load public check-ins:', error);
        if (sequence === checkinRequestSequence) {
          if (reset) list.replaceChildren();
          list.append(el('p', 'Check-ins could not be loaded. Please try again.', 'checkin-empty'));
        }
      } finally {
        if (sequence === checkinRequestSequence) {
          more.disabled = false;
          more.textContent = 'Show more';
        }
      }
    }

    function timeAgo(value) {
      const seconds = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(value).getTime()) / 1000));
      if (seconds < 60) return 'just now';
      if (seconds < 3600) return Math.floor(seconds / 60) + 'm ago';
      if (seconds < 86400) return Math.floor(seconds / 3600) + 'h ago';
      return Math.floor(seconds / 86400) + 'd ago';
    }

    async function loadActivity() {
      const panel = document.getElementById('activityPanel');
      panel.hidden = !loggedIn;
      if (!loggedIn) return;

      const res = await fetch('/api/me/activity');
      if (!res.ok) {
        document.getElementById('activityNotifications').replaceChildren(
          el('p', 'Activity could not be loaded. Please try again.', 'activity-empty'),
        );
        document.getElementById('profileVisitors').replaceChildren();
        return;
      }

      const activity = await res.json();
      const notifications = document.getElementById('activityNotifications');
      notifications.textContent = '';
      if (activity.notifications.length === 0) {
        notifications.append(el('p', 'Mentions and photo tags will show up here.', 'activity-empty'));
      } else {
        activity.notifications.forEach(item => {
          const row = el('div', undefined, 'activity-item');
          row.append(el('span', item.kind === 'photo_tag' ? 'âœ¦' : '@', 'activity-icon'));
          const copy = el('div', undefined, 'activity-copy');
          const actor = profileLink(item.actor, '@' + item.actor);
          const action = item.kind === 'photo_tag' ? ' tagged you in a photo' : ' mentioned you';
          copy.append(actor, document.createTextNode(action));
          if (item.detail) copy.append(el('div', item.detail, 'activity-detail'));
          copy.append(el('span', timeAgo(item.created_at), 'activity-time'));
          const image = el('img', undefined, 'activity-thumb');
          image.src = item.image_url;
          image.alt = 'Tagged post';
          row.append(copy, image);
          notifications.append(row);
        });
      }

      const visitors = document.getElementById('profileVisitors');
      visitors.textContent = '';
      if (activity.visitors.length === 0) {
        visitors.append(el('p', 'Your profile visitors will appear here.', 'activity-empty'));
      } else {
        activity.visitors.forEach(visitor => {
          const row = el('div', undefined, 'visitor-item');
          row.append(avatarEl(visitor.avatar_url), profileLink(visitor.username, '@' + visitor.username));
          row.append(el('span', timeAgo(visitor.last_visited_at), 'activity-time'));
          visitors.append(row);
        });
      }
    }

    window.addEventListener('hashchange', route);
    setupHomeCover();
    initCommunityMap();
    setupSidebarPeopleSearch();
    renderAuth();
  </script>
<script>
(async function loadNews() {
  const body = document.getElementById('newsBody');
  if (!body) return;
  const groups = [['local', 'India'], ['world', 'World'], ['tech', 'Tech']];
  try {
    const res = await fetch('/api/news');
    if (!res.ok) throw new Error('news ' + res.status);
    const data = await res.json();
    body.replaceChildren();
    for (const [key, label] of groups) {
      const items = (data[key] || []).slice(0, 3);
      if (!items.length) continue;
      const box = document.createElement('div');
      box.className = 'news-group';
      const h = document.createElement('h3');
      h.className = 'news-subhead';
      h.textContent = label;
      box.append(h);
      for (const it of items) {
        if (!/^https?:\/\//.test(it.link || '')) continue;
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.className = 'news-item';
        a.href = it.link;
        a.target = '_blank';
        a.rel = 'noreferrer noopener';
        a.textContent = it.title;
        if (it.source) {
          const s = document.createElement('span');
          s.className = 'news-source';
          s.textContent = it.source;
          a.append(s);
        }
        box.append(a);
      }
      body.append(box);
    }
    if (!body.children.length) throw new Error('empty');
  } catch (e) {
    body.replaceChildren();
    const p = document.createElement('p');
    p.className = 'news-note';
    p.textContent = 'Headlines are unavailable right now.';
    body.append(p);
  }
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
  const facts = [
    "Honey found in ancient Egyptian tombs was still edible after thousands of years.",
    "Octopuses have three hearts and blue blood.",
    "A day on Venus is longer than a year on Venus.",
    "Bananas are botanically berries, but strawberries are not.",
    "Wombats produce cube-shaped droppings.",
    "Sharks have been around longer than trees have.",
    "Oxford University is older than the Aztec Empire.",
    "The Eiffel Tower can grow about 15 cm taller in hot summer weather.",
    "Crows can recognize individual human faces and remember them.",
    "Chess is believed to have originated in India, from a game called chaturanga.",
    "Lightning is about five times hotter than the surface of the Sun.",
    "A group of flamingos is called a flamboyance."
  ];
  const quotes = [
    "The night does not hide the stars. It only asks you to look harder.",
    "Some storms do not come to destroy you. They come to clear the path.",
    "Shadows only exist because something is shining.",
    "Silence can be loud when the mind is full.",
    "Even the darkest room is changed by one small light.",
    "Not every ghost is dead. Some are memories that stayed.",
    "Walk through the dark long enough and your eyes learn to see.",
    "The moon has a darker side too, and nobody calls it less."
  ];
  const f = document.getElementById('funFactText');
  const q = document.getElementById('darkQuoteText');
  if (!f || !q) return;
  const day = Math.floor(Date.now() / 86400000);
  let fi = day % facts.length;
  let qi = (day * 3 + 1) % quotes.length;
  const show = () => { f.textContent = facts[fi]; q.textContent = quotes[qi]; };
  document.getElementById('funFactNext').onclick = () => { fi = (fi + 1) % facts.length; show(); };
  document.getElementById('darkQuoteNext').onclick = () => { qi = (qi + 1) % quotes.length; show(); };
  show();
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
  const MOODS = ['Bored', 'Romantic', 'Angry', 'Sad', 'Happy', 'Calm', 'Excited', 'Playful', 'Lonely', 'Grateful', 'Stressed'];
  const COLORS = ['#6a54c6', '#347e72', '#c2547a', '#d98b2b', '#2f6fb0', '#1f1b2e'];
  const tray = document.getElementById('storyTray');
  if (!tray) return;
  const me = () => (typeof currentUsername === 'string' ? currentUsername : null);
  const h = (tag, cls, text) => {
    const e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  };
  const moodEmoji = m => (typeof moodIcon === 'function' ? moodIcon(m) : '') || '';
  const avatarNode = url => {
    if (typeof avatarEl === 'function') return avatarEl(url);
    const d = h('div', 'avatar');
    return d;
  };
  const ago = iso => {
    const mins = Math.max(0, Math.floor((Date.now() - new Date(iso).getTime()) / 60000));
    if (mins < 1) return 'just now';
    if (mins < 60) return mins + 'm';
    return Math.floor(mins / 60) + 'h';
  };

  function thumbNode(it) {
    if (it.thumb_kind === 'image' && it.thumb_url) {
      const im = document.createElement('img');
      im.src = it.thumb_url; im.alt = ''; im.loading = 'lazy';
      return im;
    }
    if (it.thumb_kind === 'video' && it.thumb_url) {
      const v = document.createElement('video');
      v.src = it.thumb_url + '#t=0.1'; v.muted = true; v.preload = 'metadata'; v.playsInline = true;
      return v;
    }
    const d = h('span', 'story-thumb-text', it.thumb_body || '');
    d.style.background = it.thumb_bg || '#6a54c6';
    return d;
  }

  async function refreshTray() {
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/stories');
      if (!res.ok) { tray.hidden = true; return; }
      const items = await res.json();
      tray.replaceChildren();
      tray.hidden = false;
      const add = h('button', 'story-bubble');
      add.type = 'button';
      add.append(h('span', 'story-add-plus', '+'), h('span', 'story-name', 'Your story'));
      add.onclick = openCreator;
      tray.append(add);
      items.forEach(it => {
        const b = h('button', 'story-bubble');
        b.type = 'button';
        b.classList.add('story-card');
        const ring = h('span', 'story-thumb' + (it.all_seen ? ' seen' : ''));
        ring.append(thumbNode(it));
        ring.append(avatarNode(it.avatar_url));
        b.append(ring, h('span', 'story-name', it.username === me() ? 'You' : it.username));
        b.onclick = () => openViewer(it.username, it.avatar_url);
        tray.append(b);
      });
    } catch (e) {
      tray.hidden = true;
    }
  }
  window.refreshStoryTray = refreshTray;
  window.openStories = openViewer;

  function openShare(username, onClose) {
    const link = location.origin + '/#/story/' + encodeURIComponent(username);
    const text = '@' + username + ' shared a story on Community';
    const ov = h('div', 'share-overlay');
    const box = h('div', 'share-box');
    ov.append(box);
    const close = () => { ov.remove(); if (onClose) onClose(); };
    ov.onclick = e => { if (e.target === ov) close(); };
    box.append(h('h3', 'sc-title', 'Share this story'));
    box.append(h('p', 'share-note', 'Anyone opening the link needs a Community account, and sees the story only if its owner allows it.'));
    const addOpt = (label, fn) => {
      const b = h('button', 'share-opt', label);
      b.type = 'button';
      b.onclick = () => fn(b);
      box.append(b);
    };
    if (navigator.share) {
      addOpt('Share via your device...', async () => {
        try { await navigator.share({ title: 'Community story', text: text, url: link }); } catch (e) {}
        close();
      });
    }
    addOpt('Copy link', async b => {
      try { await navigator.clipboard.writeText(link); b.textContent = 'Link copied'; }
      catch (e) { window.prompt('Copy this link:', link); }
    });
    const enc = encodeURIComponent;
    [
      ['WhatsApp', 'https://wa.me/?text=' + enc(text + ' ' + link)],
      ['Telegram', 'https://t.me/share/url?url=' + enc(link) + '&text=' + enc(text)],
      ['X (Twitter)', 'https://twitter.com/intent/tweet?text=' + enc(text) + '&url=' + enc(link)],
      ['Facebook', 'https://www.facebook.com/sharer/sharer.php?u=' + enc(link)],
      ['Email', 'mailto:?subject=' + enc('A story on Community') + '&body=' + enc(text + ' ' + link)],
    ].forEach(([label, url]) => addOpt(label, () => {
      if (url.indexOf('mailto:') === 0) location.href = url;
      else window.open(url, '_blank', 'noopener');
    }));
    const done = h('button', 'share-opt share-close', 'Close');
    done.type = 'button';
    done.onclick = close;
    box.append(done);
    document.body.append(ov);
  }

  async function openViewer(username, avatarUrl) {
    let stories;
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/users/' + encodeURIComponent(username) + '/stories');
      if (res.status === 401) { alert('Log in to view this story.'); return; }
      if (res.status === 403) { alert('Follow this person to see their stories.'); return; }
      if (!res.ok) throw new Error('status ' + res.status);
      stories = await res.json();
    } catch (e) {
      alert('Could not load stories.');
      return;
    }
    if (!stories.length) { alert('No active stories right now.'); return; }

    const isMine = username === me();
    const ov = h('div', 'sv-overlay');
    const card = h('div', 'sv-card');
    const body = h('div', 'sv-body');
    const bars = h('div', 'sv-bars');
    const head = h('div', 'sv-head');
    const foot = h('div', 'sv-foot');
    card.append(body, bars, head, foot);
    ov.append(card);
    document.body.append(ov);
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';

    let i = Math.max(0, stories.findIndex(s => !s.viewed));
    let timer = null;

    function close() {
      clearTimeout(timer);
      document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey);
      ov.remove();
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      refreshTray();
    }
    function next() { if (i < stories.length - 1) show(i + 1); else close(); }
    function prev() { show(Math.max(0, i - 1)); }
    function onKey(e) {
      if (e.key === 'Escape') close();
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') next();
      else if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') prev();
    }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    ov.onclick = e => { if (e.target === ov) close(); };
    body.onclick = e => {
      const r = body.getBoundingClientRect();
      if (e.clientX - r.left < r.width * 0.3) prev(); else next();
    };

    function show(idx) {
      i = idx;
      clearTimeout(timer);
      body.replaceChildren();
      bars.replaceChildren();
      head.replaceChildren();
      foot.replaceChildren();
      const s = stories[i];

      const fills = stories.map((_, k) => {
        const b = h('div', 'sv-bar');
        const f = h('div', 'sv-fill');
        if (k < i) f.style.width = '100%';
        b.append(f);
        bars.append(b);
        return f;
      });

      head.append(avatarNode(avatarUrl), h('strong', null, username), h('span', null, ago(s.created_at)));
      if (s.mood) head.append(h('span', 'sv-mood', (moodEmoji(s.mood) + ' ' + s.mood).trim()));
      const shareBtn = h('button', 'sv-btn', 'Share');
      shareBtn.type = 'button';
      shareBtn.style.marginLeft = 'auto';
      shareBtn.onclick = e => {
        e.stopPropagation();
        clearTimeout(timer);
        if (video) video.pause();
        openShare(username, () => show(i));
      };
      head.append(shareBtn);
      const x = h('button', 'sv-btn', 'Close');
      x.type = 'button';
      x.style.marginLeft = '8px';
      x.onclick = e => { e.stopPropagation(); close(); };
      head.append(x);

      let video = null;
      if (s.kind === 'text') {
        body.style.background = s.bg_color;
        body.append(h('div', 'sv-text', s.body));
      } else if (s.kind === 'image') {
        body.style.background = '#000';
        const img = document.createElement('img');
        img.src = s.media_url;
        img.alt = '';
        body.append(img);
        if (s.body) body.append(h('div', 'sv-caption', s.body));
      } else {
        body.style.background = '#000';
        video = document.createElement('video');
        video.src = s.media_url;
        video.playsInline = true;
        body.append(video);
        if (s.body) body.append(h('div', 'sv-caption', s.body));
      }

      const fill = fills[i];
      if (video) {
        video.onended = next;
        video.ontimeupdate = () => {
          if (video.duration) fill.style.width = (video.currentTime / video.duration * 100) + '%';
        };
        video.play().catch(() => { video.muted = true; video.play().catch(() => {}); });
      } else {
        fill.style.transition = 'none';
        fill.style.width = '0%';
        void fill.offsetWidth;
        fill.style.transition = 'width 5000ms linear';
        fill.style.width = '100%';
        timer = setTimeout(next, 5000);
      }

      if (isMine) {
        foot.append(h('span', null, s.view_count + (s.view_count === 1 ? ' view' : ' views')));
        const del = h('button', 'sv-btn', 'Delete');
        del.type = 'button';
        del.onclick = async e => {
          e.stopPropagation();
          if (!confirm('Delete this story?')) return;
          try {
            const r = await fetch('/api/stories/' + s.id, { method: 'DELETE' });
            if (!r.ok) throw new Error('status ' + r.status);
            stories.splice(i, 1);
            if (!stories.length) close(); else show(Math.min(i, stories.length - 1));
          } catch (err) {
            console.error('Could not delete story:', err);
          }
        };
        foot.append(del);
      } else {
        fetch('/api/stories/' + s.id + '/view', { method: 'POST' }).catch(() => {});
      }
    }
    show(i);
  }

  function openCreator() {
    const state = { mode: 'text', bg: COLORS[0], mood: '', file: null };
    const ov = h('div', 'sc-overlay');
    const card = h('div', 'sc-card');
    ov.append(card);

    const tabs = h('div', 'sc-tabs');
    const tText = h('button', 'sc-tab on', 'Text');
    const tMedia = h('button', 'sc-tab', 'Photo / video');
    tText.type = tMedia.type = 'button';
    tabs.append(tText, tMedia);

    const ta = h('textarea', 'sc-text colored');
    ta.maxLength = 280;
    const count = h('div', 'sc-count', '0 / 280');
    ta.oninput = () => { count.textContent = ta.value.length + ' / 280'; };

    const swatches = h('div', 'sc-swatches');
    const sw = COLORS.map(c => {
      const b = h('button', 'sc-swatch' + (c === state.bg ? ' on' : ''));
      b.type = 'button';
      b.style.background = c;
      b.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Background ' + c);
      b.onclick = () => { state.bg = c; sw.forEach(o => o.classList.toggle('on', o === b)); paint(); };
      return b;
    });
    swatches.append(...sw);

    const fileWrap = h('div');
    const input = document.createElement('input');
    input.type = 'file';
    input.accept = 'image/jpeg,image/png,image/webp,image/gif,video/mp4,video/webm';
    const preview = h('div', 'sc-preview');
    fileWrap.append(input, preview);

    const err = h('p', 'sc-err');
    input.onchange = () => {
      err.textContent = '';
      preview.replaceChildren();
      state.file = null;
      const f = input.files[0];
      if (!f) return;
      if (f.size > 25 * 1024 * 1024) { err.textContent = 'File is too large (max 25 MB).'; input.value = ''; return; }
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(f);
      if (f.type.startsWith('video/')) {
        const v = document.createElement('video');
        v.src = url;
        v.controls = true;
        v.onloadedmetadata = () => {
          if (v.duration > 30) {
            err.textContent = 'Videos can be at most 30 seconds.';
            preview.replaceChildren();
            input.value = '';
            state.file = null;
          }
        };
        preview.append(v);
      } else {
        const im = document.createElement('img');
        im.src = url;
        im.alt = '';
        preview.append(im);
      }
      state.file = f;
    };

    const moodsLabel = h('div', 'sc-count', 'How are you feeling? (optional)');
    moodsLabel.style.textAlign = 'left';
    const moods = h('div', 'sc-moods');
    const moodBtns = MOODS.map(m => {
      const b = h('button', 'sc-mood', (moodEmoji(m) + ' ' + m).trim());
      b.type = 'button';
      b.onclick = () => {
        state.mood = state.mood === m ? '' : m;
        moodBtns.forEach(o => o.classList.toggle('on', o === b && state.mood === m));
      };
      return b;
    });
    moods.append(...moodBtns);

    const row = h('div', 'sc-row');
    const cancel = h('button', 'sc-btn', 'Cancel');
    const share = h('button', 'sc-btn primary', 'Share');
    cancel.type = share.type = 'button';
    row.append(cancel, share);

    function paint() {
      const isText = state.mode === 'text';
      tText.classList.toggle('on', isText);
      tMedia.classList.toggle('on', !isText);
      ta.classList.toggle('colored', isText);
      ta.style.background = isText ? state.bg : '';
      ta.placeholder = isText ? 'What is on your mind today?' : 'Add a caption (optional)';
      swatches.hidden = !isText;
      fileWrap.hidden = isText;
    }
    tText.onclick = () => { state.mode = 'text'; paint(); };
    tMedia.onclick = () => { state.mode = 'media'; paint(); };

    function closeCreator() { document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey); ov.remove(); }
    function onKey(e) { if (e.key === 'Escape') closeCreator(); }
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    cancel.onclick = closeCreator;
    ov.onclick = e => { if (e.target === ov) closeCreator(); };

    share.onclick = async () => {
      err.textContent = '';
      const text = ta.value.trim();
      if (state.mode === 'media' && !state.file) { err.textContent = 'Choose a photo or video first.'; return; }
      if (state.mode === 'text' && !text) { err.textContent = 'Write something first.'; return; }
      const fd = new FormData();
      fd.append('body', text);
      fd.append('bg_color', state.bg);
      if (state.mood) fd.append('mood', state.mood);
      if (state.mode === 'media' && state.file) fd.append('media', state.file);
      share.disabled = true;
      share.textContent = 'Sharing...';
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/stories', { method: 'POST', body: fd });
        if (res.status === 401) throw new Error('Please log in again.');
        if (res.status === 413) throw new Error('File is too large (max 25 MB).');
        if (res.status === 415) throw new Error('That file type is not supported.');
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Could not share (' + res.status + ').');
        closeCreator();
        refreshTray();
      } catch (e) {
        err.textContent = e.message;
        share.disabled = false;
        share.textContent = 'Share';
      }
    };

    card.append(h('h3', 'sc-title', 'Share how you feel today'), tabs, ta, count, swatches, fileWrap, moodsLabel, moods, err, row);
    paint();
    document.body.append(ov);
  }

  refreshTray();
  setInterval(refreshTray, 30000);

  function openFromHash() {
    const m = location.hash.match(/^#\/story\/([^\/?#]+)/);
    if (!m) return;
    const name = decodeURIComponent(m[1]);
    history.replaceState(null, '', location.pathname + location.search + '#/');
    openViewer(name, null);
  }
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', openFromHash);
  openFromHash();
  window.addEventListener('hashchange', refreshTray);
})();
</script>
<script>
(function () {
  const h = (tag, cls, text) => {
    const e = document.createElement(tag);
    if (cls) e.className = cls;
    if (text != null) e.textContent = text;
    return e;
  };
  const fab = h('button', 'fb-fab', 'Feedback');
  fab.type = 'button';
  fab.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Send feedback');
  const topNav = document.querySelector('.top-nav');
  if (topNav) { fab.classList.add('in-nav'); topNav.append(fab); } else { document.body.append(fab); }

  fab.onclick = () => {
    const loggedIn = typeof currentUsername === 'string' && currentUsername;
    if (!loggedIn) { alert('Log in to send feedback.'); return; }
    const cats = [['bug', 'Bug'], ['idea', 'Idea'], ['praise', 'Praise'], ['other', 'Other']];
    let category = 'idea';
    const ov = h('div', 'fb-overlay');
    const card = h('div', 'fb-card');
    ov.append(card);

    const catRow = h('div', 'fb-cats');
    const catBtns = cats.map(([key, label]) => {
      const b = h('button', 'fb-cat' + (key === category ? ' on' : ''), label);
      b.type = 'button';
      b.onclick = () => { category = key; catBtns.forEach(o => o.classList.toggle('on', o === b)); };
      return b;
    });
    catRow.append(...catBtns);

    const ta = h('textarea', 'fb-text');
    ta.maxLength = 1000;
    ta.placeholder = 'What happened, or what would you like to see? The more specific, the better.';
    const count = h('span', null, '0 / 1000');
    ta.oninput = () => { count.textContent = ta.value.length + ' / 1000'; };
    const meta = h('div', 'fb-meta');
    meta.append(h('span', null, 'We record the page you are on and your browser type.'), count);

    const status = h('p', 'fb-status');
    const row = h('div', 'fb-row');
    const cancel = h('button', 'fb-btn', 'Cancel');
    const send = h('button', 'fb-btn primary', 'Send');
    cancel.type = send.type = 'button';
    row.append(cancel, send);

    const close = () => { document.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey); ov.remove(); };
    const onKey = e => { if (e.key === 'Escape') close(); };
    document.addEventListener('keydown', onKey);
    cancel.onclick = close;
    ov.onclick = e => { if (e.target === ov) close(); };

    send.onclick = async () => {
      const message = ta.value.trim();
      status.style.color = '#b4423f';
      if (message.length < 3) { status.textContent = 'Please write a little more.'; return; }
      send.disabled = true;
      status.textContent = '';
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/feedback', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ category, message, page: location.hash || '#/' }),
        });
        if (res.status === 401) throw new Error('Please log in again.');
        if (res.status === 429) throw new Error('That is a lot of feedback. Please try again in a while.');
        if (!res.ok) throw new Error('Could not send (' + res.status + ').');
        status.style.color = '#347e72';
        status.textContent = 'Thank you! Your feedback was sent.';
        setTimeout(close, 1300);
      } catch (e) {
        status.textContent = e.message;
        send.disabled = false;
      }
    };

    card.append(h('h3', 'fb-title', 'Send feedback'), catRow, ta, meta, status, row);
    document.body.append(ov);
    ta.focus();
  };
})();
</script>
</body>
</html>

    CONSTRAINT users_cover_position_y_check CHECK (((cover_position_y >= 0) AND (cover_position_y <= 100))),
    CONSTRAINT users_cover_zoom_check CHECK (((cover_zoom >= 100) AND (cover_zoom <= 200))),
    CONSTRAINT users_mood_check CHECK (((mood IS NULL) OR (mood = ANY (ARRAY['Bored'::text, 'Romantic'::text, 'Angry'::text, 'Sad'::text, 'Happy'::text, 'Calm'::text, 'Excited'::text, 'Playful'::text, 'Lonely'::text, 'Grateful'::text, 'Stressed'::text]))))
);


ALTER TABLE public.users OWNER TO postgres;

--
-- Name: users_id_seq; Type: SEQUENCE; Schema: public; Owner: postgres
--

CREATE SEQUENCE public.users_id_seq
    AS integer
    START WITH 1
    INCREMENT BY 1
    NO MINVALUE
    NO MAXVALUE
    CACHE 1;


ALTER SEQUENCE public.users_id_seq OWNER TO postgres;
